4/-!
5# Commutator estimates, coercivity and dynamical modulated-energy bounds (package ENER-02)
6
7Source: HRS periodic main paper, Section 3.5 `B:sec:blackbox` (source lines 2800-3281):
8* `B:eq:commutator-quantity` (2815-2820): `Comm_N(v; X_N, μ)`.
9* Proposition 3.32 `M:B:prop:commutator` (2824-2836), external: Chodron de Courcel–Rosenzweig–
10  Serfaty, Prop. 2.13 (`s ≥ d-2`) and Hess-Childs–Rosenzweig, Thm. 1.1 & Rem. 1.3 (`s < d-2`).
11* Proposition 3.33 `M:B:prop:coercivity` (2842-2852).
12* Proposition 3.34 `M:B:prop:hrs-periodic-coercivity` (2865-2898).
13* Remark 3.35 `M:B:rem:hrs-coercivity-fixed-density` (3056-3066).
14* Proposition 3.36 `M:B:prop:gronwall-catalog` (3078-3111).
15* Proposition 3.37 `M:B:prop:lowtemp` (3113-3124).
16* Proposition 3.38 `M:B:prop:comparison-kernel-identity` (3155-3167).
17* Remark 3.39 `M:B:rem:MF-truncated` (3177-3194).
18* Proposition 3.41 `M:B:prop:mfe-uniform` (3204-3217).
19* Remarks 3.40, 3.42, 3.43, 3.44, 3.45: no formal content (see `coverage.tsv`).
20* The transport seminorm `‖v‖_*` is `B:eq:comm-norm` (10163).
21
22## Representation decisions (recorded in `docs/ISSUES.md` §B, ENER-02)
23
24* **Density bounds.** `‖μ‖_∞ ≤ Λ` is stated for the chosen density representative `ρ` as a
25  pointwise bound `∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ` (an `L^∞` bound always has such a representative); `μ = ρ dx`
26  through `withDensityReal`.  The smearing scale is `λ = (NΛ)^{-1/d}` (`smearScale`), as in
27  Proposition 3.34; the condition `λ < 1` is automatic for `N ≥ 2`, `Λ ≥ 1`.
28* **The correction `o_N`.** All statements use the exact correction of Proposition 3.34,
29  `o_N(Λ) = 1_{s=0} log(NΛ)/(2dN) + C₀ Λ^{s/d} N^{s/d-1}` (`oNΛ`,
30  `B:eq:hrs-coercivity-exact-correction`); Remark 3.35 relates it to `oN` of
31  `H:eq:energy-completion` (`HRS.oN`), which has the same logarithmic coefficient and absorbs the
32  `Λ`-dependence into `C₀`.
33* **`‖∇v‖_{L^∞}` for Lipschitz `v`** is the Lipschitz constant with respect to the torus distance
34  (`IsLipschitzVF`), which equals the essential supremum of `|∇v|`; statements quantify over all
35  admissible constants.  The fractional part of `‖v‖_*` is represented through a function `w`
36  with the Fourier coefficients of `|∇|^{(d+1)/2} v` (`IsFracDeriv`) and its `L^{2d/(d-1)}` norm
37  (`eLpNorm`, in `ℝ≥0∞`); `HasTransportBound d s v K` says `‖v‖_* ≤ K`.  This avoids `tsum`
38  junk values for non-summable Fourier series.
39* **Off-diagonal integrals** against `(μ_N - μ)^{⊗2}` are written out term by term with the
40  diagonal deleted explicitly (`if x = y then 0 else …`), as `interactionPairing` does; the
41  source asserts absolute convergence (lines 2820-2821), and Proposition 3.32 asserts it in its
42  conclusion so that no Bochner integral is a junk value.
43* **The modulated free energy `𝖤_N(f,μ)` and the normalised relative entropy `𝖧_N`** are used
44  in Propositions 3.36(C2) and 3.41 without a definition in the main paper (see ISSUES §C); they
45  are fixed here as `𝖧_N(f|Q) = N⁻¹ H(f|Q)` and
46  `𝖤_N(f,μ) = β⁻¹ 𝖧_N(f | μ^{⊗N}) + ∫ F_N(X_N, μ) f(dX_N)`, the only reading consistent with
47  lines 3230-3234 and 3102.  Finiteness is a separate predicate (`MFEFinite`), asserted in the
48  conclusions.
49* **Expectations of energies** are stated as `lintegral`s of `ENNReal.ofReal (F_N + o_N)`
50  (nonnegative by coercivity) on the left and Bochner integrals with an explicit integrability
51  hypothesis at time `0` on the right, so that finiteness is part of the claim.
52* **Proposition 3.38** is stated in integrated form `E F^t - E F^0 = ∫_0^t (…) dr` (the content of
53  the `d/dt` identity obtained from Itô's formula), with the integrability of the microscopic
54  term as a hypothesis (the source presupposes the displayed expectations exist).
55-/
56
57noncomputable section

64variable {d N : ℕ}

66/-! ### Corrections, scales and seminorms -/
67
68/-- The exact correction `o_N(Λ) = 1_{s=0} log(NΛ)/(2dN) + C₀ Λ^{s/d} N^{s/d-1}`
69(`B:eq:hrs-coercivity-exact-correction`, source lines 2884-2887). -/
70def oNΛ (d : ℕ) (s C₀ Λ : ℝ) (N : ℕ) : ℝ :=
71  (if s = 0 then Real.log (N * Λ) / (2 * d * N) else 0) +
72    C₀ * Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)
73
74/-- `a_N^Λ = Λ^{s/d} N^{s/d-1}` (`B:eq:hrs-coercivity-correction`, line 2881). -/
75def aNΛ (d : ℕ) (s Λ : ℝ) (N : ℕ) : ℝ := Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)
76
77/-- `a_N = (1 + 1_{s=0} log N) N^{s/d-1}` (Remark 3.35, line 3060). -/
78def aN (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (N : ℕ) : ℝ :=
79  (1 + if s = 0 then Real.log N else 0) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)
80
81/-- The smearing scale `λ = (NΛ)^{-1/d}` (lines 2826, 2844, 2881). -/
82def smearScale (d : ℕ) (N : ℕ) (Λ : ℝ) : ℝ := (N * Λ) ^ (-(1 : ℝ) / d)
83
84/-- A Lipschitz vector field with constant `L` (so `‖∇v‖_{L^∞} ≤ L`), with respect to the
85Euclidean torus distance `tdist`. -/
86def IsLipschitzVF (L : ℝ) (v : Torus d → Euc d) : Prop :=
87  ∀ x y, ‖v x - v y‖ ≤ L * KernelGrad.tdist x y
88
89/-- `w = |∇|^a v` componentwise: `ŵ_j(k) = (2π|k|)^a v̂_j(k)` for all `k ∈ ℤ^d`. -/
90def IsFracDeriv (a : ℝ) (v w : Torus d → Euc d) : Prop :=
91  ∀ (j : Fin d) (k : Lattice d),
92    Torus.fcoef (fun x => w x j) k = ((2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ a : ℝ) * Torus.fcoef (fun x => v x j) k
93
94/-- `‖v‖_* ≤ K` for the transport seminorm `B:eq:comm-norm` (source line 10163):
95`‖v‖_* = ‖∇v‖_{L^∞} + 1_{s<d-2} ‖|∇|^{(d+1)/2} v‖_{L^{2d/(d-1)}}`. -/
96def HasTransportBound (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (v : Torus d → Euc d) (K : ℝ) : Prop :=
97  ∃ L F : ℝ, 0 ≤ L ∧ 0 ≤ F ∧ IsLipschitzVF L v ∧
98    (s < d - 2 → ∃ w : Torus d → Euc d, IsFracDeriv (((d : ℝ) + 1) / 2) v w ∧
99      eLpNorm w (ENNReal.ofReal (2 * d / ((d : ℝ) - 1))) volume ≤ ENNReal.ofReal F) ∧
100    L + (if s < d - 2 then F else 0) ≤ K
101
102/-- The squared homogeneous Sobolev seminorm `‖ψ‖²_{Ḣ^a(𝕋^d)} = ∑_{k≠0} (2π|k|)^{2a} |ψ̂(k)|²`
103(source line 2851 with `a = (d-s)/2`). -/
104def homSobolevSq (a : ℝ) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) : ℝ :=
105  ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k.1) ^ (2 * a) * ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k.1‖ ^ 2
106
107/-- Finiteness of the homogeneous Sobolev seminorm (summability of its defining series; without
108it the real `tsum` is the junk value `0`). -/
109def HasFiniteHomSobolev (a : ℝ) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) : Prop :=
110  Summable fun k : NonzeroLattice d =>
111    (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k.1) ^ (2 * a) * ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k.1‖ ^ 2
112
113/-- `‖ψ‖_{Ḣ^a}`. -/
114def homSobolev (a : ℝ) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) : ℝ := Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq a ψ)
115
116/-! ### The commutator quantity -/
117
118/-- The symmetrised commutator kernel `(v(x) - v(y)) · G(x - y)` off the diagonal, `0` on it. -/
119def commKernel (G : Torus d → Euc d) (v : Torus d → Euc d) (x y : Torus d) : ℝ :=
120  if x = y then 0 else inner ℝ (v x - v y) (G (x - y))
121
122/-- `∬_{𝔻²∖△} k d(μ_N - μ)^{⊗2}` for a symmetric kernel `k`, written out with the particle diagonal
123deleted: `N⁻² ∑_{i≠j} k(x_i,x_j) - 2N⁻¹ ∑_i ∫ k(x_i,y) dμ(y) + ∬ k dμ dμ` (the kernel is taken to
124vanish on the diagonal, which is `μ ⊗ μ`- and `μ_N ⊗ μ`-null for `μ ≪ dx`). -/
125def offDiagPairing (k : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) : ℝ :=
126  (1 / (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, k (X i) (X j)
127    - (2 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i, ∫ y, k (X i) y ∂μ
128    + ∫ x, ∫ y, k x y ∂μ ∂μ
129
130/-- `Comm_N^G(v; X_N, μ) = ∬_{𝔻²∖△} (v(x)-v(y)) · G(x-y) d(N⁻¹∑δ_{x_i} - μ)^{⊗2}` for a force field
131`G` (`G = ∇g` in `B:eq:commutator-quantity`, `G = ∇h` for the comparison kernel of
132Proposition 3.38). -/
133def commQuantityWith (G : Torus d → Euc d) (v : Torus d → Euc d) (X : Config d N)
134    (μ : Measure (Torus d)) : ℝ :=
135  offDiagPairing (commKernel G v) X μ
136
137/-- `Comm_N(v; X_N, μ)` of `B:eq:commutator-quantity` (source lines 2815-2818), for the periodic
138Riesz/log kernel. -/
139def commQuantity (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (v : Torus d → Euc d) (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) :
140    ℝ :=
141  commQuantityWith (periodicRieszGrad d s) v X μ
142
143/-- Absolute convergence of the integrals defining `offDiagPairing k X μ` (lines 2820-2821). -/
144def OffDiagIntegrable (k : Torus d → Torus d → ℝ) (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) :
145    Prop :=
146  (∀ i, Integrable (fun y => k (X i) y) μ) ∧
147    Integrable (fun p : Torus d × Torus d => k p.1 p.2) (μ.prod μ)
148
149/-! ### Proposition 3.32: commutator estimate -/
150
151/-- Proposition 3.32 `M:B:prop:commutator` (source lines 2824-2836), item (i) (torus):
152there are constants `C₀ = C₀(d,s)` and `C` such that, for every `0 ≤ s < d`, every probability
153density `μ` with `‖μ‖_∞ ≤ Λ` (`λ = (NΛ)^{-1/d} < 1`), every pairwise distinct `X_N` and every
154Lipschitz vector field `v` with `‖v‖_* ≤ K`, the integrals defining `Comm_N(v; X_N, μ)` converge
155absolutely and `|Comm_N(v;X_N,μ)| ≤ C ‖v‖_* (F_N(X_N,μ) + o_N)`, pathwise.
156
157External: for `s ≥ d-2` this is Chodron de Courcel–Rosenzweig–Serfaty, *Sharp commutator
158estimates of all order for Coulomb and Riesz modulated energies* (2025), Prop. 2.13; for
159`s < d-2` Hess-Childs–Rosenzweig, *Sharp commutator estimates …* (2025), Thm. 1.1 and Rem. 1.3,
160with the normalisation translation of `H:subsec:conventions` (lines 417-430; the kernel here is
161`periodicRiesz d s`, ISSUES §B K1).  PLAN: the source gives no proof; a Lean proof must follow the
162cited papers (periodic Riesz: smearing at scale `λ` with the heat-kernel/Caffarelli–Silvestre
163extension, commutator with the stress tensor, the `s<d-2` case through the fractional transport
164term of `‖v‖_*`).  The absolute-convergence clause follows from `|commKernel| ≤ C L (|x-y|^{-s} + 1)`
165(`periodicRieszGrad` bounds in `HRS/Kernel/GradBasic.lean`/`GradReg.lean`) and `s < d`. -/
166theorem commutator_estimate (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
167    ∃ C₀ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₀ ∧ 0 < C ∧
168      ∀ (Λ : ℝ), 1 ≤ Λ → ∀ (N : ℕ), 2 ≤ N →
169      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
170      ∀ X : Config d N, X ∉ collisionSet d N →
171      ∀ (v : Torus d → Euc d) (K : ℝ), HasTransportBound d s v K →
172        OffDiagIntegrable (commKernel (periodicRieszGrad d s) v) X (withDensityReal ρ) ∧
173        |commQuantity d s v X (withDensityReal ρ)| ≤
174          C * K * (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N) := by
175  -- ENER-02b route decision: this is the external input of Prop. 3.32 (Chodron de Courcel-Rosenzweig-
176  -- Serfaty Prop. 2.13; Hess-Childs-Rosenzweig Thm 1.1).  Neither the repository (`HRS/Energy`,
177  -- `HRS/Kernel`) nor the vendored `external/dgrhs` (attractive-log partition bounds, CLT; no
178  -- commutator layer) contains the smearing/stress-tensor commutator machinery, so it stays an
179  -- "external" `sorry` (docs/ISSUES.md Q8).
180  sorry
181
182/-! ### Propositions 3.33 and 3.34: coercivity -/
183
184/-! ### Route for `periodic_coercivity` (package ENER-02c; helpers in `HRS.CoercAux`)
185
186With `a = (d-s)/2`, `A = subA d s`, `psiFn x u = ∑_{k≠0} e^{-u|k|²} cos(2π k·x)`:
187* `g(x) = A ∫_0^∞ u^{a-1} psiFn x u du` for `x ≠ 0`; split at `u = ε` (`ε = λ²`):
188  `g = gT ε + nearF ε` with `gT ε = ∑_k cT ε k cos(2π k·x)`, `cT ε k = A ∫_ε^∞ u^{a-1} e^{-u|k|²} du ≥ 0`
189  (`periodicRiesz_decomp`, `gT_eq_integral`), the TeX truncation `g_η`.
190* `nearF ≥ -A ε^a/a` (since `psiFn ≥ -1`: positivity of theta, `psi_ge_neg_one`), `∫ nearF = 0`
191  (both `g` and `gT` have mean zero); hence `∫ nearF(x-y) dμ(y) ∈ [-B', ΛB']` (`nearF_conv_bounds`).
192* `Esq = ∬ gT d(μ_N-μ)^{⊗2} = ∑_k cT |\hat{ρ}(k)|² ≥ 0` (`Q_eq_tsum`, `integral_trigSum_sub`), and
193  `F_N ≥ ½ Esq - gT(0)/(2N) - (1+Λ)B'` (`rieszEnergy_ge_aux`); `gT(0) ≲ ε^{-s/2}` (`s>0`) resp.
194  `½ log(1/ε) + C` (`s=0`, exact coefficient `subA d 0 · π^{d/2} = ½`: `subA_zero_mul`), via the
195  small-time theta bound `one_add_psi_zero_le`.  With `ε = λ²`: `Esq ≤ 2 (F_N + o_N)` (`coercivity_energy`).
196* Test bound: mollify `ψ` by the periodic heat kernel `p_ε` (`heatK`, mass one, first moment
197  `≲ √ε` from the Gaussian majorant `Gt`; `heat_moment`, `heatP_approx`), and use Fourier
198  Cauchy–Schwarz (`fourier_side_bound`) with the lower bound `cT ε k ≳ e^{-2ε|k|²}|k|^{-(d-s)}`
199  (`cT_lower`, `intT_lower`).  The case `d = 1` needs `periodicRiesz` facts that the library only
200  proves for `2 ≤ d` (`*_one` copies in `CoercCopies`, the proofs never use `2 ≤ d`). -/
201

211theorem rieszConst_pos_one {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
212    0 < rieszConst d s := by
213  have hd' : (0:ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
214  unfold rieszConst
215  split_ifs with h
216  · exact mul_pos (mul_pos (by positivity) (by positivity)) (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by positivity))
217  · have hs : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm h)
218    exact div_pos (mul_pos (mul_pos (by positivity) (by positivity))
219      (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by linarith))) (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by linarith))
220
221theorem periodicRiesz_tendsto_one (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (x : Torus d) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
222    Tendsto (fun ε => heatRiesz d s ε x) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 (periodicRiesz d s x)) := by
223  obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := heat_limit_exists hsd x hx
224  have : periodicRiesz d s x = L := hL.limUnder_eq
225  rw [this]; exact hL
226
227theorem integral_periodicRiesz_mode_one {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
228    (k : Lattice d) :
229    ∫ x : Torus d, Torus.mode (-k) x * ((periodicRiesz d s x : ℝ) : ℂ) = ((rieszCoeff d s k : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
230  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
231  have : Nonempty (Fin d) := ⟨⟨0, hd0⟩⟩
232  obtain ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, hbd⟩ := heat_eps_bound hs0 hsd
233  set μ : Measure (Fin d → ℝ) := volume.restrict (Box d) with hμ
234  set G : (Fin d → ℝ) → ℝ := fun y => C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2 with hG
235  set Fε : ℝ → (Fin d → ℝ) → ℂ := fun ε y =>
236    Torus.mode (-k) (boxPt y) * ((heatRiesz d s ε (boxPt y) : ℝ) : ℂ) with hFε
237  set F0 : (Fin d → ℝ) → ℂ := fun y =>
238    Torus.mode (-k) (boxPt y) * ((periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y) : ℝ) : ℂ) with hF0
239  have hne : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, y ≠ 0 := by
240    have h1 : ∀ᵐ y ∂(volume : Measure (Fin d → ℝ)), y ≠ 0 := by
241      rw [ae_iff]; simp
242    exact ae_restrict_of_ae h1
243  have hmem : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, y ∈ Box d := ae_restrict_mem (measurableSet_Box d)
244  have hdom := tendsto_integral_filter_of_dominated_convergence (μ := μ) (l := 𝓝[>] (0:ℝ))
245    (F := Fε) (f := F0) G ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
246  · -- identify the limit
247    have h1 : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∫ y, Fε ε y ∂μ =
248        ((rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
249      intro ε hε
250      rw [← integral_heat_mode hsd hε k]
251      exact (integral_torus_box (fun x : Torus d => Torus.mode (-k) x * ((heatRiesz d s ε x : ℝ) : ℂ))).symm
252    have h2 : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => ((rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ))
253        (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 ((rieszCoeff d s k : ℝ) : ℂ)) := by
254      have : Continuous fun ε : ℝ => ((rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
255        fun_prop
256      have := (this.tendsto 0).mono_left (nhdsWithin_le_nhds (s := Ioi (0:ℝ)))
257      simpa using this
258    have h3 : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => ∫ y, Fε ε y ∂μ) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 ((rieszCoeff d s k : ℝ) : ℂ)) :=
259      h2.congr' (by filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε using (h1 ε hε).symm)
260    have h4 := tendsto_nhds_unique hdom h3
261    rw [← h4]
262    exact integral_torus_box (fun x : Torus d => Torus.mode (-k) x * ((periodicRiesz d s x : ℝ) : ℂ))
263  · filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε
264    have hε' : 0 < ε := hε
265    refine Continuous.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
266    exact ((Torus.continuous_mode _).comp (continuous_boxPt d)).mul
267      (Complex.continuous_ofReal.comp ((KernelAux.continuous_heatRiesz hsd hε').comp (continuous_boxPt d)))
268  · filter_upwards [Ioo_mem_nhdsGT one_pos] with ε hε
269    filter_upwards [hne, hmem] with y hy hyB
270    have hy0 : boxPt y ≠ 0 := fun h => hy (boxPt_eq_zero hyB h)
271    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := exists_argmax hd0 y
272    have hyj : y j ≠ 0 := by
273      intro h0
274      apply hy
275      have : ‖y‖ = 0 := by rw [hj, h0, abs_zero]
276      exact norm_eq_zero.mp this
277    have := hbd y (Box_abs_le hyB) j hyj ε hε.1 hε.2.le
278    simp only [hFε, norm_mul, norm_mode, one_mul, Complex.norm_real, Real.norm_eq_abs, hG, hj]
279    exact this
280  · have hI : IntegrableOn (fun y : Fin d → ℝ => ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2))) (Box d) :=
281      integrableOn_Box_rpow hd0 (by linarith)
282    exact (hI.const_mul C1).add (integrableOn_const (measure_Box_ne_top d))
283  · filter_upwards [hne, hmem] with y hy hyB
284    have hy0 : boxPt y ≠ 0 := fun h => hy (boxPt_eq_zero hyB h)
285    have := periodicRiesz_tendsto_one hd hs0 hsd (boxPt y) hy0
286    exact (Complex.continuous_ofReal.tendsto _ |>.comp this).const_mul _
287

289theorem periodicRiesz_box_bound_one {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
290    ∃ C1 C2 : ℝ, 0 ≤ C1 ∧ 0 ≤ C2 ∧ ∀ y : Fin d → ℝ, y ∈ Box d → y ≠ 0 →
291      |periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y)| ≤ C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2 := by
292  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
293  obtain ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, hbd⟩ := heat_eps_bound hs0 hsd
294  refine ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, fun y hyB hy => ?_⟩
295  have hy0 : boxPt y ≠ 0 := fun h => hy (boxPt_eq_zero hyB h)
296  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := exists_argmax hd0 y
297  have hyj : y j ≠ 0 := by
298    intro h0
299    apply hy
300    have : ‖y‖ = 0 := by rw [hj, h0, abs_zero]
301    exact norm_eq_zero.mp this
302  have hlim := (periodicRiesz_tendsto_one hd hs0 hsd (boxPt y) hy0).abs
303  refine le_of_tendsto hlim ?_
304  filter_upwards [Ioo_mem_nhdsGT one_pos] with ε hε
305  have := hbd y (Box_abs_le hyB) j hyj ε hε.1 hε.2.le
306  rwa [← hj] at this
307
308theorem aestronglyMeasurable_periodicRiesz_one {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
309    AEStronglyMeasurable (periodicRiesz d s) (volume : Measure (Torus d)) := by
310  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
311  refine aestronglyMeasurable_of_tendsto_ae (u := atTop) (f := fun n : ℕ => heatRiesz d s (1 / ((n:ℝ) + 1)))
312    (fun n => (KernelAux.continuous_heatRiesz hsd (by positivity)).aestronglyMeasurable) ?_
313  filter_upwards [torus_ae_ne hd0] with x hx
314  have h := periodicRiesz_tendsto_one hd hs0 hsd x hx
315  refine h.comp ?_
316  refine tendsto_nhdsWithin_iff.mpr ⟨tendsto_one_div_add_atTop_nhds_zero_nat, Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun n => ?_⟩
317  simp only [mem_Ioi]; positivity
318
319theorem integrable_periodicRiesz_one {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
320    Integrable (periodicRiesz d s) := by
321  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
322  obtain ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, hbd⟩ := periodicRiesz_box_bound_one hd hs0 hsd
323  refine ⟨aestronglyMeasurable_periodicRiesz_one hd hs0 hsd, ?_⟩
324  unfold HasFiniteIntegral
325  rw [lintegral_torus_box (fun x => ‖periodicRiesz d s x‖ₑ)]
326  have hI : IntegrableOn (fun y : Fin d → ℝ => C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2) (Box d) :=
327    ((integrableOn_Box_rpow hd0 (by linarith)).const_mul C1).add
328      (integrableOn_const (measure_Box_ne_top d))
329  have hne : ∀ᵐ y ∂(volume.restrict (Box d)), y ≠ 0 := by
330    exact ae_restrict_of_ae (ae_ne_pi hd0)
331  calc ∫⁻ y in Box d, ‖periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y)‖ₑ
332      ≤ ∫⁻ y in Box d, ENNReal.ofReal (C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2) := by
333        refine lintegral_mono_ae ?_
334        filter_upwards [hne, ae_restrict_mem (measurableSet_Box d)] with y hy hyB
335        rw [← ofReal_norm, Real.norm_eq_abs]
336        exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hbd y hyB hy)
337    _ = ∫⁻ y in Box d, ‖C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2‖ₑ := by
338        refine lintegral_congr fun y => ?_
339        rw [Real.enorm_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
340    _ < ∞ := hI.2
341
342theorem periodicRiesz_eq_integral_one {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
343    (x : Torus d) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
344    periodicRiesz d s x = subA d s * ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn x t := by
345  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
346  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
347  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := psi_bound x hx
348  have hcont := psi_continuousOn x
349  have hmeas : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 ≤ ε → AEStronglyMeasurable (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x (t + ε))
350      (volume.restrict (Ioi 0)) := by
351    intro ε hε
352    refine ContinuousOn.aestronglyMeasurable ?_ measurableSet_Ioi
353    refine ContinuousOn.mul (continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt ht)) ?_
354    refine hcont.comp (continuousOn_id.add continuousOn_const) fun t ht => ?_
355    simp only [mem_Ioi] at ht ⊢
356    linarith
357  have hlim : Tendsto (fun ε => ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x (t + ε)) (𝓝[>] 0)
358      (𝓝 (∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x t)) := by
359    refine tendsto_integral_filter_of_dominated_convergence
360      (fun t => M * (Real.exp (-t) * t ^ (a - 1))) ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
361    · filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε using hmeas ε (le_of_lt hε)
362    · filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε
363      refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
364      have hε' : 0 < ε := hε
365      have ht' : 0 < t := ht
366      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' _)]
367      have h1 := hM (t + ε) (by linarith)
368      have h2 : Real.exp (-(t + ε)) ≤ Real.exp (-t) := Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (by linarith)
369      have h3 := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' (a - 1)
370      calc t ^ (a - 1) * |psiFn x (t + ε)| ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (M * Real.exp (-t)) := by
371            gcongr
372            exact h1.trans (by gcongr)
373        _ = _ := by ring
374    · exact (Real.GammaIntegral_convergent ha).const_mul M
375    · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
376      have ht' : 0 < t := ht
377      refine Tendsto.const_mul _ ?_
378      have h1 : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => t + ε) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 t) := by
379        have : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => t + ε) (𝓝 0) (𝓝 (t + 0)) :=
380          (continuous_const.add continuous_id).tendsto 0
381        rw [add_zero] at this
382        exact this.mono_left nhdsWithin_le_nhds
383      have h2 : ContinuousAt (psiFn x) t :=
384        (hcont t ht').continuousAt (Ioi_mem_nhds ht')
385      exact h2.tendsto.comp h1
386  have h2 : Tendsto (fun ε => heatRiesz d s ε x) (𝓝[>] 0)
387      (𝓝 (subA d s * ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x t)) := by
388    refine (hlim.const_mul _).congr' ?_
389    filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε
390    exact (heatRiesz_eq_integral hsd hε x).symm
391  exact tendsto_nhds_unique (periodicRiesz_tendsto_one hd hs0 hsd x hx) h2
392
393
394theorem subA_nonneg_one {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) : 0 ≤ subA d s := by
395  unfold subA
396  have := rieszConst_pos_one hd hs0 hsd
397  have := Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (show 0 < ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 by linarith)
398  have : 0 < (2 * π) ^ (s - d) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by positivity) _
399  positivity
400
401theorem integral_periodicRiesz_one (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
402    Integrable (periodicRiesz d s) ∧ ∫ x, periodicRiesz d s x = 0 := by
403  refine ⟨integrable_periodicRiesz_one hd hs0 hsd, ?_⟩
404  have h := integral_periodicRiesz_mode_one hd hs0 hsd 0
405  simp only [neg_zero, Torus.mode_zero, one_mul] at h
406  rw [integral_complex_ofReal] at h
407  have h0 : rieszCoeff d s 0 = 0 := by simp [rieszCoeff]
408  rw [h0] at h
409  exact_mod_cast h

415section CoercTrunc

417open KernelAux Set
418
419variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}
420
421/-- The Fourier coefficient of the truncated kernel `g_ε`:
422`A ∫_ε^∞ u^{a-1} e^{-u|k|²} du` (`k ≠ 0`), `a = (d-s)/2`, `A = subA d s`. -/
423def cT (d : ℕ) (s ε : ℝ) (k : Lattice d) : ℝ :=
424  if k = 0 then 0 else
425    subA d s * ∫ u in Ioi ε, u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u))
426
427/-- The truncated kernel `g_ε = ∑_k ĉ_ε(k) cos(2π k·x)`. -/
428def gT (d : ℕ) (s ε : ℝ) (x : Torus d) : ℝ := ∑' k : Lattice d, cT d s ε k * Torus.cosMode k x
429
430theorem integrableOn_T {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (k : Lattice d) (hk : k ≠ 0) :
431    IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u))) (Ioi ε) :=
432  (integrable_majorant ha k hk).mono_set (Ioi_subset_Ioi hε.le)
433
434theorem integrableOn_half {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) :
435    IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(1 / 2 * u))) (Ioi 0) := by
436  have := integrableOn_rpow_mul_exp_neg_mul_rpow (p := 1) (s := a - 1) (b := 1 / 2)
437    (by linarith) one_pos (by norm_num)
438  refine this.congr_fun (fun t _ => ?_) measurableSet_Ioi
439  simp only [Real.rpow_one]
440  ring_nf
441
442/-- the constant `∫_0^∞ u^{a-1} e^{-u/2} du`. -/
443def halfInt (a : ℝ) : ℝ := ∫ u in Ioi (0:ℝ), u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(1 / 2 * u))
444
445theorem intT_le {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (k : Lattice d) (hk : k ≠ 0) :
446    ∫ u in Ioi ε, u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u)) ≤
447      Real.exp (-(ε / 2) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * halfInt a := by
448  have hm : 1 ≤ Lattice.enorm k := one_le_enorm k hk
449  have hm2 : 1 ≤ Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 := by nlinarith
450  have hint := ((integrableOn_half ha).mono_set (Ioi_subset_Ioi hε.le)).const_mul
451    (Real.exp (-(ε / 2) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2))
452  calc ∫ u in Ioi ε, u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u))
453      ≤ ∫ u in Ioi ε, Real.exp (-(ε / 2) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
454          (u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(1 / 2 * u))) := by
455        refine setIntegral_mono_on (integrableOn_T ha hε k hk) hint measurableSet_Ioi ?_
456        intro u hu
457        have hu' : ε < u := hu
458        have hu0 : 0 < u := by linarith
459        have h1 : 0 ≤ u ^ (a - 1) := (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 _).le
460        have h2 : Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u)) ≤
461            Real.exp (-(ε / 2) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(1 / 2 * u)) := by
462          rw [← Real.exp_add]
463          apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
464          nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hm2 hu0.le]
465        calc u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u))
466            ≤ u ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-(ε / 2) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(1 / 2 * u))) :=
467              mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 h1
468          _ = _ := by ring
469    _ = Real.exp (-(ε / 2) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
470          ∫ u in Ioi ε, u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(1 / 2 * u)) := by rw [integral_const_mul]
471    _ ≤ _ := by
472        unfold halfInt
473        refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (Real.exp_pos _).le
474        refine setIntegral_mono_set (integrableOn_half ha) ?_ (Ioi_subset_Ioi hε.le).eventuallyLE
475        refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u hu => ?_)
476        have hu0 : (0:ℝ) < u := hu
477        simp only [Pi.zero_apply]
478        have := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 (a - 1)
479        positivity
480
481
482
483
484theorem halfInt_nonneg (a : ℝ) : 0 ≤ halfInt a := by
485  unfold halfInt
486  refine setIntegral_nonneg measurableSet_Ioi fun u hu => ?_
487  have hu0 : (0:ℝ) < u := hu
488  have := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 (a - 1)
489  positivity
490
491theorem cT_nonneg (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (k : Lattice d) :
492    0 ≤ cT d s ε k := by
493  unfold cT
494  split_ifs with hk
495  · exact le_rfl
496  · refine mul_nonneg (subA_nonneg_one hd hs0 hsd) ?_
497    refine setIntegral_nonneg measurableSet_Ioi fun u hu => ?_
498    have hu0 : (0:ℝ) < u := lt_trans hε hu
499    have := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1)
500    positivity
501
502theorem cT_le (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (k : Lattice d) :
503    cT d s ε k ≤ subA d s * halfInt (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2) * Real.exp (-(ε / 2) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := by
504  have ha : 0 < ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 := by linarith
505  have hA := subA_nonneg_one hd hs0 hsd
506  have hH := halfInt_nonneg (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2)
507  unfold cT
508  split_ifs with hk
509  · positivity
510  · have := intT_le ha hε k hk
511    calc subA d s * ∫ u in Ioi ε, u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u))
512        ≤ subA d s * (Real.exp (-(ε / 2) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * halfInt (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2)) :=
513          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this hA
514      _ = _ := by ring
515
516theorem summable_cT (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
517    Summable (cT d s ε) := by
518  refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (cT_nonneg hd hs0 hsd hε) (cT_le hd hs0 hsd hε)
519    ((summable_gauss_lattice d (ε / 2) (by positivity)).mul_left _)
520
521theorem continuous_gT (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
522    Continuous (gT d s ε) := by
523  unfold gT
524  refine continuous_tsum (u := cT d s ε) (fun k => continuous_const.mul (Torus.continuous_cosMode k))
525    (summable_cT hd hs0 hsd hε) (fun k x => ?_)
526  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (cT_nonneg hd hs0 hsd hε k)]
527  calc cT d s ε k * |Torus.cosMode k x| ≤ cT d s ε k * 1 :=
528        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (abs_cosMode_le k x) (cT_nonneg hd hs0 hsd hε k)
529    _ = _ := mul_one _

534/-- The integrand of the `k`-th mode of the truncated representation. -/
535def fT (x : Torus d) (a : ℝ) (k : Lattice d) (u : ℝ) : ℝ :=
536  u ^ (a - 1) * (if k = 0 then 0 else Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x)
537
538theorem fT_bound (x : Torus d) (a : ℝ) (k : Lattice d) (hk : k ≠ 0) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) :
539    ‖fT x a k u‖ ≤ u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u)) := by
540  unfold fT
541  simp only [hk, ite_false, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _),
542    abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu _)]
543  have hc := abs_cosMode_le k x
544  have hp : 0 ≤ u ^ (a - 1) := (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu _).le
545  have e : Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) = Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u)) := by
546    congr 1; ring
547  rw [e]
548  calc u ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u)) * |Torus.cosMode k x|)
549      ≤ u ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u)) * 1) := by gcongr
550    _ = _ := by ring
551
552theorem fT_integrable {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (x : Torus d) (k : Lattice d) :
553    IntegrableOn (fT x a k) (Ioi ε) := by
554  by_cases hk : k = 0
555  · have : fT x a k = 0 := by funext u; simp [fT, hk]
556    rw [this]; exact integrableOn_zero
557  · refine Integrable.mono' (integrableOn_T ha hε k hk) ?_ ?_
558    · refine Measurable.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
559      unfold fT
560      simp only [hk, ite_false]
561      fun_prop
562    · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u hu => ?_)
563      exact fT_bound x a k hk (lt_trans hε hu)
564
565theorem gT_eq_integral (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (x : Torus d) :
566    gT d s ε x = subA d s * ∫ u in Ioi ε, u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn x u := by
567  set a : ℝ := ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
568  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
569  have hH := halfInt_nonneg a
570  have hFnorm : Summable fun k : Lattice d => ∫ u in Ioi ε, ‖fT x a k u‖ := by
571    refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => integral_nonneg fun u => norm_nonneg _)
572      (fun k => ?_) ((summable_gauss_lattice d (ε / 2) (by positivity)).mul_left (halfInt a))
573    by_cases hk : k = 0
574    · have : ∀ u, ‖fT x a k u‖ = 0 := fun u => by simp [fT, hk]
575      simp only [this, integral_zero]
576      exact mul_nonneg hH (Real.exp_pos _).le
577    · calc ∫ u in Ioi ε, ‖fT x a k u‖
578          ≤ ∫ u in Ioi ε, u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u)) := by
579            refine setIntegral_mono_on (fT_integrable ha hε x k).norm (integrableOn_T ha hε k hk)
580              measurableSet_Ioi ?_
581            intro u hu
582            exact fT_bound x a k hk (lt_trans hε hu)
583        _ ≤ Real.exp (-(ε / 2) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * halfInt a := intT_le ha hε k hk
584        _ = _ := by ring
585  have hsw : ∑' k : Lattice d, ∫ u in Ioi ε, fT x a k u =
586      ∫ u in Ioi ε, ∑' k : Lattice d, fT x a k u :=
587    integral_tsum_of_summable_integral_norm (fT_integrable ha hε x) hFnorm
588  have hterm : ∀ k : Lattice d, cT d s ε k * Torus.cosMode k x =
589      subA d s * ∫ u in Ioi ε, fT x a k u := by
590    intro k
591    by_cases hk : k = 0
592    · have : fT x a k = 0 := by funext u; simp [fT, hk]
593      have h0 : cT d s ε k = 0 := by simp [cT, hk]
594      rw [h0, zero_mul, this]
595      simp
596    · have h1 : (fun u => fT x a k u) = fun u =>
597          (u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u))) * Torus.cosMode k x := by
598        funext u
599        simp only [fT, hk, ite_false]
600        have e : Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) = Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * u)) := by
601          congr 1; ring
602        rw [e]; ring
603      rw [h1, integral_mul_const]
604      simp only [cT, hk, ite_false]
605      ring
606  have hpt : ∀ u : ℝ, 0 < u → ∑' k : Lattice d, fT x a k u = u ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x u := by
607    intro u hu
608    unfold fT psiFn
609    rw [tsum_mul_left]
610  unfold gT
611  simp_rw [hterm]
612  rw [tsum_mul_left, hsw]
613  congr 1
614  exact setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioi fun u hu => hpt u (lt_trans hε hu)

619theorem integral_Ioc_rpow {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
620    ∫ u in Ioc 0 ε, u ^ (a - 1) = ε ^ a / a := by
621  rw [← intervalIntegral.integral_of_le hε.le, integral_rpow (Or.inl (by linarith))]
622  simp [Real.zero_rpow ha.ne']
623
624theorem integrableOn_Ioc_rpow {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
625    IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (a - 1)) (Ioc 0 ε) := by
626  have := intervalIntegral.intervalIntegrable_rpow' (a := 0) (b := ε) (r := a - 1) (by linarith)
627  exact (intervalIntegrable_iff_integrableOn_Ioc_of_le hε.le).mp this

629theorem psi_ge_neg_one (x : Torus d) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) : -1 ≤ psiFn x u := by
630  have h := theta_nonneg x hu
631  rw [psi_eq x hu] at h
632  linarith

634theorem periodicRiesz_decomp (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε)
635    (x : Torus d) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
636    periodicRiesz d s x = gT d s ε x +
637      subA d s * ∫ u in Ioc 0 ε, u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn x u := by
638  rw [periodicRiesz_eq_integral_one hd hs0 hsd x hx, gT_eq_integral hd hs0 hsd hε x, ← mul_add]
639  congr 1
640  rw [← Ioc_union_Ioi_eq_Ioi hε.le, setIntegral_union Ioc_disjoint_Ioi_same measurableSet_Ioi]
641  · rw [add_comm]
642  · exact (integrableOn_psi hsd x hx).mono_set (by
643      intro u hu; exact hu.1 |> fun h => (mem_Ioi.mpr h))
644  · exact (integrableOn_psi hsd x hx).mono_set (Ioi_subset_Ioi hε.le)
645
646theorem near_ge (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε)
647    (x : Torus d) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
648    -(subA d s * (ε ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2))) ≤
649      periodicRiesz d s x - gT d s ε x := by
650  have ha : 0 < ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 := by linarith
651  have hA := subA_nonneg_one hd hs0 hsd
652  rw [periodicRiesz_decomp hd hs0 hsd hε x hx, add_sub_cancel_left]
653  have hint : IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn x u) (Ioc 0 ε) :=
654    (integrableOn_psi hsd x hx).mono_set (fun u hu => mem_Ioi.mpr hu.1)
655  have hI : -(ε ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2)) ≤
656      ∫ u in Ioc 0 ε, u ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn x u := by
657    rw [← integral_Ioc_rpow ha hε, ← integral_neg]
658    refine setIntegral_mono_on (integrableOn_Ioc_rpow ha hε).neg hint measurableSet_Ioc ?_
659    intro u hu
660    have h0 : 0 < u := hu.1
661    have h1 := psi_ge_neg_one x h0
662    have h2 := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos h0 (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1)
663    nlinarith
664  calc -(subA d s * (ε ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2)))
665      = subA d s * (-(ε ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2))) := by ring
666    _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hI hA

671/-- constant in the theta bound -/
672def thT : ℝ := ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / 2) * (n : ℝ) ^ 2)
673
674theorem thT_nonneg : 0 ≤ thT := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
675
676/-- `S(u) = ∑_n e^{-π² n²/u} ≤ 1 + (2 thT/π²) u` for `0 < u ≤ 1`. -/
677theorem theta_zero_one_dim {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) (hu1 : u ≤ 1) :
678    ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 * ((n : ℝ) - 0) ^ 2 / u)) ≤ 1 + (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u := by
679  set E := Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * u))) with hE
680  have hsum : Summable fun n : ℤ => Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / 2) * (n : ℝ) ^ 2) :=
681    summable_exp_sq _ (by positivity)
682  have hb : ∀ n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 * ((n : ℝ) - 0) ^ 2 / u)) ≤
683      (if n = 0 then 1 else 0) + E * Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / 2) * (n : ℝ) ^ 2) := by
684    intro n
685    by_cases hn : n = 0
686    · subst hn
687      have hE0 : 0 ≤ E := (Real.exp_pos _).le
688      simp
689      positivity
690    · simp only [hn, ite_false, zero_add]
691      rw [← Real.exp_add]
692      apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
693      have h1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (n : ℝ) ^ 2 := by
694        have := Int.one_le_abs hn
695        have h2 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ |(n : ℝ)| := by exact_mod_cast this
696        nlinarith [sq_abs (n : ℝ)]
697      have hπ : 0 < Real.pi ^ 2 := by positivity
698      have h3 : (n : ℝ) ^ 2 / u ≥ 1 / (2 * u) + (n : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2 := by
699        rw [ge_iff_le, div_add_div _ _ (by positivity) (by norm_num), div_le_div_iff₀ (by positivity) hu]
700        nlinarith [mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.mpr h1) (sub_nonneg.mpr hu1), sq_nonneg (n : ℝ), hu.le,
701          mul_nonneg hu.le (sub_nonneg.mpr h1)]
702      have h4 : Real.pi ^ 2 * ((n : ℝ) - 0) ^ 2 / u = Real.pi ^ 2 * ((n : ℝ) ^ 2 / u) := by ring
703      rw [h4]
704      have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h3 hπ.le
705      have h5 : Real.pi ^ 2 * (1 / (2 * u) + (n : ℝ) ^ 2 / 2) =
706          Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * u) + Real.pi ^ 2 / 2 * (n : ℝ) ^ 2 := by ring
707      rw [h5] at this
708      nlinarith
709  have hsum1 : Summable fun n : ℤ => Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 * ((n : ℝ) - 0) ^ 2 / u)) := by
710    refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun n => (Real.exp_pos _).le) hb ?_
711    exact (summable_of_ne_finset_zero (s := {0}) (by intro n hn; simp at hn; simp [hn])).add
712      (hsum.mul_left E)
713  calc ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 * ((n : ℝ) - 0) ^ 2 / u))
714      ≤ ∑' n : ℤ, ((if n = 0 then 1 else 0) + E * Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / 2) * (n : ℝ) ^ 2)) :=
715        hsum1.tsum_le_tsum hb ((summable_of_ne_finset_zero (s := {0})
716          (by intro n hn; simp at hn; simp [hn])).add (hsum.mul_left E))
717    _ = 1 + E * thT := by
718        rw [(summable_of_ne_finset_zero (s := {0})
719          (by intro n hn; simp at hn; simp [hn])).tsum_add (hsum.mul_left E), tsum_mul_left]
720        simp [thT]
721    _ ≤ _ := by
722        have hv : 0 < Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * u) := by positivity
723        have h1 : E ≤ 2 * u / Real.pi ^ 2 := by
724          rw [hE, Real.exp_neg]
725          have := Real.add_one_le_exp (Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * u))
726          rw [inv_le_comm₀ (Real.exp_pos _) (by positivity)]
727          have h2 : (Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * u)) ≤ Real.exp (Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * u)) := by linarith
728          calc (2 * u / Real.pi ^ 2)⁻¹ = Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * u) := by rw [inv_div]
729            _ ≤ _ := h2
730        have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h1 thT_nonneg
731        calc 1 + E * thT ≤ 1 + 2 * u / Real.pi ^ 2 * thT := by linarith
732          _ = _ := by ring
733

737theorem exp_le_one_add_mul (x : ℝ) : Real.exp x ≤ 1 + x * Real.exp x := by
738  have h := Real.add_one_le_exp (-x)
739  have h2 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h (Real.exp_pos x).le
740  rw [mul_add, ← Real.exp_add, add_neg_cancel, Real.exp_zero, mul_one, mul_neg] at h2
741  linarith
742
743/-- the constant `c₂` in `1 + Ψ_0(u) ≤ π^{d/2} u^{-d/2} (1 + c₂ u)` -/
744def thC (d : ℕ) : ℝ := d * (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * Real.exp (d * (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2))
745
746theorem thC_nonneg (d : ℕ) : 0 ≤ thC d := by
747  unfold thC
748  have := thT_nonneg
749  positivity
750
751theorem one_add_psi_zero_le (d : ℕ) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) (hu1 : u ≤ 1) :
752    1 + psiFn (0 : Torus d) u ≤ Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) * (1 + thC d * u) := by
753  have h := psi_eq (0 : Torus d) hu
754  have hb : (boxPt (0 : Fin d → ℝ) : Torus d) = 0 := by
755    funext i; simp [boxPt]
756  have h2 := theta_prod (0 : Fin d → ℝ) hu
757  rw [hb] at h2
758  rw [h2] at h
759  simp only [Pi.zero_apply] at h
760  rw [Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin] at h
761  rw [← h]
762  set S := ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 * ((n : ℝ) - 0) ^ 2 / u)) with hS
763  have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
764  have hS1 := theta_zero_one_dim hu hu1
765  rw [← hS] at hS1
766  have hc1 : 0 ≤ 2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2 := by have := thT_nonneg; positivity
767  have h3 : (Real.sqrt (Real.pi / u) * S) ^ d ≤ (Real.sqrt (Real.pi / u) * (1 + (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u)) ^ d :=
768    pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hS1 (Real.sqrt_nonneg _)) d
769  refine h3.trans ?_
770  rw [mul_pow, sqrt_div_pow_eq d hu]
771  have h4 : (1 + (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u) ^ d ≤ 1 + thC d * u := by
772    have e1 : (1 + (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u) ≤ Real.exp ((2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u) := by
773      have := Real.add_one_le_exp ((2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u); linarith
774    have e2 : (1 + (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u) ^ d ≤ Real.exp (d * ((2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u)) := by
775      rw [Real.exp_nat_mul]
776      exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) e1 d
777    refine e2.trans ?_
778    have e3 := exp_le_one_add_mul (d * ((2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u))
779    refine e3.trans ?_
780    have e4 : Real.exp (d * ((2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u)) ≤ Real.exp (d * (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2)) := by
781      apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
782      have : (0:ℝ) ≤ d * (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) := by positivity
783      nlinarith
784    unfold thC
785    have : (0:ℝ) ≤ d * ((2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u) := by positivity
786    calc 1 + ↑d * (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2 * u) * Real.exp (↑d * (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2 * u))
787        ≤ 1 + ↑d * (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2 * u) * Real.exp (↑d * (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2)) := by gcongr
788      _ = _ := by ring
789  have h5 : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) := by positivity
790  calc Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) * (1 + (2 * thT / Real.pi ^ 2) * u) ^ d
791      ≤ Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) * (1 + thC d * u) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h4 h5
792

796theorem psi_zero_nonneg {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) : 0 ≤ psiFn (0 : Torus d) u := by
797  unfold psiFn
798  refine tsum_nonneg fun k => ?_
799  split_ifs
800  · exact le_rfl
801  · have : Torus.cosMode k (0 : Torus d) = 1 := by
802      simp [Torus.cosMode, Torus.mode, UnitAddTorus.mFourier]
803    rw [this, mul_one]
804    exact (Real.exp_pos _).le

806/-- the constant in the large-`u` bound of `Ψ`. -/
807def psiLargeC (d : ℕ) : ℝ := Real.exp 1 * ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)
808
809theorem psiLargeC_nonneg (d : ℕ) : 0 ≤ psiLargeC d := by
810  unfold psiLargeC
811  have : 0 ≤ ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) :=
812    tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
813  positivity

815theorem gT_zero_le (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (hε1 : ε ≤ 1) :
816    gT d s ε 0 ≤ subA d s * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d *
817        (∫ u in Ioc ε 1, u ^ (-s / 2 - 1) * (1 + thC d * u)) +
818      psiLargeC d * Real.Gamma (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2)) := by
819  set a : ℝ := ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
820  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
821  have hA := subA_nonneg_one hd hs0 hsd
822  rw [gT_eq_integral hd hs0 hsd hε 0]
823  refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hA
824  set f : ℝ → ℝ := fun u => u ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (0 : Torus d) u with hf
825  have hcont : ContinuousOn f (Ioi 0) :=
826    ContinuousOn.mul (continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt ht)) (psi_continuousOn _)
827  have hint1 : IntegrableOn f (Ioc ε 1) := by
828    have : ContinuousOn f (Icc ε 1) := hcont.mono (fun u hu => lt_of_lt_of_le hε hu.1)
829    exact this.integrableOn_Icc.mono_set Ioc_subset_Icc_self
830  have hG : IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => psiLargeC d * (Real.exp (-u) * u ^ (a - 1))) (Ioi 0) :=
831    (Real.GammaIntegral_convergent ha).const_mul (psiLargeC d)
832  have hbound : ∀ u ∈ Ioi (1:ℝ), f u ≤ psiLargeC d * (Real.exp (-u) * u ^ (a - 1)) := by
833    intro u hu
834    have hu1 : (1:ℝ) < u := hu
835    have hu0 : 0 < u := by linarith
836    have := psi_large (0 : Torus d) hu1.le
837    have h2 := (abs_le.mp this).2
838    have h3 := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 (a - 1)
839    rw [hf]
840    unfold psiLargeC
841    calc u ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (0 : Torus d) u
842        ≤ u ^ (a - 1) * ((Real.exp 1 * ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) *
843            Real.exp (-u)) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 h3.le
844      _ = _ := by ring
845  have hint2 : IntegrableOn f (Ioi 1) := by
846    refine Integrable.mono' (hG.mono_set (Ioi_subset_Ioi zero_le_one)) ?_ ?_
847    · exact (hcont.mono (Ioi_subset_Ioi zero_le_one)).aestronglyMeasurable measurableSet_Ioi
848    · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u hu => ?_)
849      have hu1 : (1:ℝ) < u := hu
850      have hu0 : 0 < u := by linarith
851      have h0 : 0 ≤ f u := by
852        rw [hf]
853        exact mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 _).le (psi_zero_nonneg hu0)
854      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg h0]
855      exact hbound u hu
856  have hsplit : ∫ u in Ioi ε, f u = (∫ u in Ioc ε 1, f u) + ∫ u in Ioi 1, f u := by
857    rw [← Ioc_union_Ioi_eq_Ioi hε1, setIntegral_union Ioc_disjoint_Ioi_same measurableSet_Ioi hint1 hint2]
858  rw [hsplit]
859  refine add_le_add ?_ ?_
860  · calc ∫ u in Ioc ε 1, f u
861        ≤ ∫ u in Ioc ε 1, Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * (u ^ (-s / 2 - 1) * (1 + thC d * u)) := by
862          refine setIntegral_mono_on hint1 ?_ measurableSet_Ioc ?_
863          · refine Integrable.const_mul ?_ _
864            have : ContinuousOn (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (-s / 2 - 1) * (1 + thC d * u)) (Icc ε 1) := by
865              refine ContinuousOn.mul ?_ (by fun_prop)
866              exact continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt (lt_of_lt_of_le hε ht.1))
867            exact this.integrableOn_Icc.mono_set Ioc_subset_Icc_self
868          · intro u hu
869            have hu0 : 0 < u := lt_of_lt_of_le hε hu.1.le
870            have h1 := one_add_psi_zero_le d hu0 hu.2
871            have h2 : psiFn (0 : Torus d) u ≤ Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) * (1 + thC d * u) := by
872              linarith
873            have h3 := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 (a - 1)
874            have h4 : u ^ (a - 1) * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) = u ^ (-s / 2 - 1) := by
875              rw [← Real.rpow_add hu0]; congr 1; rw [ha_def]; ring
876            rw [hf]
877            calc u ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (0 : Torus d) u
878                ≤ u ^ (a - 1) * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) * (1 + thC d * u)) :=
879                  mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 h3.le
880              _ = Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * (u ^ (a - 1) * u ^ (-(d : ℝ) / 2) * (1 + thC d * u)) := by ring
881              _ = _ := by rw [h4]
882      _ = _ := by rw [integral_const_mul]
883  · calc ∫ u in Ioi 1, f u
884        ≤ ∫ u in Ioi (1:ℝ), psiLargeC d * (Real.exp (-u) * u ^ (a - 1)) :=
885          setIntegral_mono_on hint2 (hG.mono_set (Ioi_subset_Ioi zero_le_one)) measurableSet_Ioi hbound
886      _ ≤ ∫ u in Ioi (0:ℝ), psiLargeC d * (Real.exp (-u) * u ^ (a - 1)) := by
887          refine setIntegral_mono_set hG ?_ (Ioi_subset_Ioi zero_le_one).eventuallyLE
888          refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u hu => ?_)
889          have hu0 : (0:ℝ) < u := hu
890          have := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 (a - 1)
891          have := psiLargeC_nonneg d
892          simp only [Pi.zero_apply]
893          positivity
894      _ = _ := by rw [integral_const_mul, Real.Gamma_eq_integral ha]

899theorem subA_zero_mul (hd : 1 ≤ d) : subA d 0 * Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d = 1 / 2 := by
900  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
901  have hΓ : 0 < Real.Gamma ((d : ℝ) / 2) := Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by positivity)
902  have hπ : 0 < Real.pi := Real.pi_pos
903  unfold subA rieszConst
904  simp only [if_true, sub_zero]
905  have e1 : Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d = Real.pi ^ ((d : ℝ) / 2) := by
906    rw [Real.sqrt_eq_rpow, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hπ.le]; congr 1; ring
907  have e2 : (2 * Real.pi) ^ (-(d : ℝ)) = (2 : ℝ) ^ (-(d : ℝ)) * Real.pi ^ (-(d : ℝ)) :=
908    Real.mul_rpow (by norm_num) hπ.le
909  have e3 : (2 : ℝ) ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1) = (2 : ℝ) ^ (d : ℝ) / 2 := by
910    rw [Real.rpow_sub (by norm_num), Real.rpow_one]
911  have e4 : Real.pi ^ ((d : ℝ) / 2) * Real.pi ^ ((d : ℝ) / 2) = Real.pi ^ (d : ℝ) := by
912    rw [← Real.rpow_add hπ]; congr 1; ring
913  have e5 : (2 : ℝ) ^ (d : ℝ) * (2 : ℝ) ^ (-(d : ℝ)) = 1 := by
914    rw [← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num)]; simp
915  have e6 : Real.pi ^ (d : ℝ) * Real.pi ^ (-(d : ℝ)) = 1 := by
916    rw [← Real.rpow_add hπ]; simp
917  rw [e1, show (0:ℝ) - d = -(d : ℝ) by ring, e2, e3]
918  field_simp
919  have := e4
920  have h7 : (2 : ℝ) ^ (d : ℝ) * Real.pi ^ ((d : ℝ) / 2) * Real.pi ^ ((d : ℝ) / 2) *
921      ((2 : ℝ) ^ (-(d : ℝ)) * Real.pi ^ (-(d : ℝ))) = 1 := by
922    calc _ = ((2 : ℝ) ^ (d : ℝ) * (2 : ℝ) ^ (-(d : ℝ))) *
923          (Real.pi ^ ((d : ℝ) / 2) * Real.pi ^ ((d : ℝ) / 2) * Real.pi ^ (-(d : ℝ))) := by ring
924      _ = 1 := by rw [e5, e4, e6]; ring
925  linear_combination h7

930theorem int_main_pos (hs : 0 < s) {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 ≤ c) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (hε1 : ε ≤ 1) :
931    ∫ u in Ioc ε 1, u ^ (-s / 2 - 1) * (1 + c * u) ≤ (1 + c) * (2 / s) * ε ^ (-s / 2) := by
932  have hcont : ContinuousOn (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (-s / 2 - 1)) (Icc ε 1) :=
933    continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt (lt_of_lt_of_le hε ht.1))
934  have hint : IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (-s / 2 - 1)) (Ioc ε 1) :=
935    hcont.integrableOn_Icc.mono_set Ioc_subset_Icc_self
936  have h1 : ∫ u in Ioc ε 1, u ^ (-s / 2 - 1) * (1 + c * u) ≤ ∫ u in Ioc ε 1, (1 + c) * u ^ (-s / 2 - 1) := by
937    refine setIntegral_mono_on ?_ (hint.const_mul _) measurableSet_Ioc ?_
938    · have : ContinuousOn (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (-s / 2 - 1) * (1 + c * u)) (Icc ε 1) :=
939        hcont.mul (by fun_prop)
940      exact this.integrableOn_Icc.mono_set Ioc_subset_Icc_self
941    · intro u hu
942      have hu0 : 0 < u := lt_of_lt_of_le hε hu.1.le
943      have h3 := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 (-s / 2 - 1)
944      have : 1 + c * u ≤ 1 + c := by nlinarith [hu.2]
945      nlinarith
946  refine h1.trans ?_
947  rw [integral_const_mul, ← intervalIntegral.integral_of_le hε1, integral_rpow
948    (Or.inr ⟨by linarith, by rw [Set.uIcc_of_le hε1]; exact fun h => absurd h.1 (not_le.mpr hε)⟩)]
949  have e : (-s / 2 - 1 + 1) = -s / 2 := by ring
950  rw [e, Real.one_rpow]
951  have hs2 : 0 < s / 2 := by positivity
952  have h5 : 0 ≤ ε ^ (-s / 2) := Real.rpow_nonneg hε.le _
953  have : (1 - ε ^ (-s / 2)) / (-s / 2) = (ε ^ (-s / 2) - 1) / (s / 2) := by
954    field_simp; ring
955  rw [this]
956  have h6 : (ε ^ (-s / 2) - 1) / (s / 2) ≤ 2 / s * ε ^ (-s / 2) := by
957    rw [div_le_iff₀ hs2]
958    have : 2 / s * ε ^ (-s / 2) * (s / 2) = ε ^ (-s / 2) := by field_simp
959    rw [this]; linarith
960  calc (1 + c) * ((ε ^ (-s / 2) - 1) / (s / 2)) ≤ (1 + c) * (2 / s * ε ^ (-s / 2)) :=
961        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h6 (by linarith)
962    _ = _ := by ring
963
964theorem int_main_zero {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 ≤ c) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (hε1 : ε ≤ 1) :
965    ∫ u in Ioc ε 1, u ^ (-(0 : ℝ) / 2 - 1) * (1 + c * u) ≤ Real.log (1 / ε) + c := by
966  have e : ∀ u : ℝ, u ^ (-(0 : ℝ) / 2 - 1) = u⁻¹ := by
967    intro u; rw [show (-(0 : ℝ) / 2 - 1) = -1 by norm_num, Real.rpow_neg_one]
968  simp_rw [e]
969  have hform : ∀ u ∈ Ioc ε 1, u⁻¹ * (1 + c * u) = u⁻¹ + c := by
970    intro u hu
971    have : u ≠ 0 := ne_of_gt (lt_of_lt_of_le hε hu.1.le)
972    field_simp
973  rw [setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc hform, ← intervalIntegral.integral_of_le hε1,
974    intervalIntegral.integral_add, integral_inv_of_pos hε one_pos]
975  · simp only [intervalIntegral.integral_const, smul_eq_mul]
976    have : c * (1 - ε) ≤ c := by nlinarith
977    linarith
978  · exact intervalIntegral.intervalIntegrable_inv (f := fun u : ℝ => u) (fun u hu => by
979      rw [Set.uIcc_of_le hε1] at hu; exact ne_of_gt (lt_of_lt_of_le hε hu.1))
980      continuousOn_id
981  · exact intervalIntegrable_const
982
983
984
985
986theorem gT_zero_le_pos (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs : 0 < s) (hsd : s < d) :
987    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε ≤ 1 → gT d s ε 0 ≤ C * ε ^ (-s / 2) := by
988  have hA := subA_nonneg_one hd hs.le hsd
989  have ha : 0 < ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 := by linarith
990  have hΓ := (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos ha).le
991  have hP : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d := by positivity
992  have hc := thC_nonneg d
993  have hL := psiLargeC_nonneg d
994  refine ⟨subA d s * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * ((1 + thC d) * (2 / s)) +
995    psiLargeC d * Real.Gamma (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2)), by positivity, fun ε hε hε1 => ?_⟩
996  refine (gT_zero_le hd hs.le hsd hε hε1).trans ?_
997  have h1 := int_main_pos hs hc hε hε1
998  have h2 : 1 ≤ ε ^ (-s / 2) :=
999    Real.one_le_rpow_of_pos_of_le_one_of_nonpos hε hε1 (by linarith)
1000  have h3 : 0 ≤ psiLargeC d * Real.Gamma (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) := mul_nonneg hL hΓ
1001  calc subA d s * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * (∫ u in Ioc ε 1, u ^ (-s / 2 - 1) * (1 + thC d * u)) +
1002          psiLargeC d * Real.Gamma (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2))
1003      ≤ subA d s * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * ((1 + thC d) * (2 / s) * ε ^ (-s / 2)) +
1004          psiLargeC d * Real.Gamma (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) * ε ^ (-s / 2)) := by
1005        refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (add_le_add (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 hP) ?_) hA
1006        nlinarith
1007    _ = _ := by ring
1008
1009theorem gT_zero_le_zero (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hsd : (0 : ℝ) < d) :
1010    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε ≤ 1 → gT d 0 ε 0 ≤ 1 / 2 * Real.log (1 / ε) + C := by
1011  have hA := subA_nonneg_one hd le_rfl hsd
1012  have ha : 0 < ((d : ℝ) - 0) / 2 := by linarith
1013  have hΓ := (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos ha).le
1014  have hc := thC_nonneg d
1015  have hL := psiLargeC_nonneg d
1016  have hAP := subA_zero_mul hd
1017  refine ⟨1 / 2 * thC d + subA d 0 * (psiLargeC d * Real.Gamma (((d : ℝ) - 0) / 2)), fun ε hε hε1 => ?_⟩
1018  refine (gT_zero_le hd le_rfl hsd hε hε1).trans ?_
1019  have h1 := int_main_zero hc hε hε1
1020  have hP : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d := by positivity
1021  have h3 : subA d 0 * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * ∫ u in Ioc ε 1, u ^ (-(0:ℝ) / 2 - 1) * (1 + thC d * u)) ≤
1022      subA d 0 * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * (Real.log (1 / ε) + thC d)) :=
1023    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 hP) hA
1024  have h4 : subA d 0 * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * (Real.log (1 / ε) + thC d)) =
1025      1 / 2 * (Real.log (1 / ε) + thC d) := by
1026    calc _ = (subA d 0 * Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d) * (Real.log (1 / ε) + thC d) := by ring
1027      _ = _ := by rw [hAP]
1028  calc subA d 0 * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * (∫ u in Ioc ε 1, u ^ (-(0:ℝ) / 2 - 1) * (1 + thC d * u)) +
1029        psiLargeC d * Real.Gamma (((d : ℝ) - 0) / 2))
1030      = subA d 0 * (Real.sqrt Real.pi ^ d * (∫ u in Ioc ε 1, u ^ (-(0:ℝ) / 2 - 1) * (1 + thC d * u))) +
1031        subA d 0 * (psiLargeC d * Real.Gamma (((d : ℝ) - 0) / 2)) := by ring
1032    _ ≤ 1 / 2 * (Real.log (1 / ε) + thC d) + subA d 0 * (psiLargeC d * Real.Gamma (((d : ℝ) - 0) / 2)) := by
1033        rw [← h4]; linarith
1034    _ = _ := by ring
1035
1036
1037
1038
1039end CoercTrunc

1047/-- trigonometric sum `∑_k c_k cos(2πk·x)`. -/
1048def trigSum (c : Lattice d → ℝ) (x : Torus d) : ℝ := ∑' k : Lattice d, c k * Torus.cosMode k x
1049
1050theorem mode_sub_conj (k : Lattice d) (x y : Torus d) :
1051    Torus.mode k (x - y) = Torus.mode k x * conj (Torus.mode k y) := by
1052  have h : Torus.mode k x = Torus.mode k (x - y) * Torus.mode k y := by
1053    rw [← Torus.mode_add_arg]; simp
1054  have h1 : Torus.mode k y * conj (Torus.mode k y) = 1 := by
1055    rw [Complex.mul_conj, Complex.normSq_eq_norm_sq, norm_mode]; simp
1056  calc Torus.mode k (x - y) = Torus.mode k (x - y) * (Torus.mode k y * conj (Torus.mode k y)) := by
1057        rw [h1, mul_one]
1058    _ = _ := by rw [h]; ring
1059
1060theorem cosMode_sub (k : Lattice d) (x y : Torus d) :
1061    Torus.cosMode k (x - y) = (Torus.mode k x * conj (Torus.mode k y)).re := by
1062  unfold Torus.cosMode
1063  rw [mode_sub_conj]

1065theorem integral_tsum_c {c : Lattice d → ℝ} (hc : Summable fun k => |c k|) (P : Measure (Torus d))
1066    [IsFiniteMeasure P] (f : Lattice d → Torus d → ℝ) (hf : ∀ k, Continuous (f k)) {B : ℝ}
1067    (hb : ∀ k x, |f k x| ≤ B) :
1068    ∫ x, ∑' k, c k * f k x ∂P = ∑' k, c k * ∫ x, f k x ∂P := by
1069  have hint : ∀ k, Integrable (fun x => c k * f k x) P := fun k =>
1070    ((hf k).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)).const_mul _
1071  have hnorm : Summable fun k => ∫ x, ‖c k * f k x‖ ∂P := by
1072    refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => integral_nonneg fun x => norm_nonneg _)
1073      (fun k => ?_) (hc.mul_right (B * (P Set.univ).toReal))
1074    calc ∫ x, ‖c k * f k x‖ ∂P ≤ ∫ x, |c k| * B ∂P := by
1075          refine integral_mono (hint k).norm (integrable_const _) fun x => ?_
1076          rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul]
1077          exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hb k x) (abs_nonneg _)
1078      _ = |c k| * (B * (P Set.univ).toReal) := by
1079          rw [integral_const, smul_eq_mul, Measure.real]; ring
1080  rw [← integral_tsum_of_summable_integral_norm hint hnorm]
1081  refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
1082  rw [integral_const_mul]
1083
1084theorem integral_trigSum_sub {c : Lattice d → ℝ} (hc : Summable fun k => |c k|) (P Q : Measure (Torus d))
1085    [IsFiniteMeasure P] [IsFiniteMeasure Q] :
1086    ∫ x, ∫ y, trigSum c (x - y) ∂Q ∂P =
1087      ∑' k, c k * ((∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂P) * conj (∫ y, Torus.mode k y ∂Q)).re := by
1088  have hmint : ∀ (k : Lattice d) (R : Measure (Torus d)) [IsFiniteMeasure R],
1089      Integrable (Torus.mode k) R := fun k R _ =>
1090    (Torus.continuous_mode k).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
1091  have hinner : ∀ x, ∫ y, trigSum c (x - y) ∂Q =
1092      ∑' k, c k * (Torus.mode k x * conj (∫ y, Torus.mode k y ∂Q)).re := by
1093    intro x
1094    have h1 : ∀ y, trigSum c (x - y) = ∑' k, c k * (Torus.mode k x * conj (Torus.mode k y)).re := by
1095      intro y; unfold trigSum; simp_rw [cosMode_sub]
1096    simp_rw [h1]
1097    rw [integral_tsum_c hc Q (fun k y => (Torus.mode k x * conj (Torus.mode k y)).re)
1098      (fun k => by
1099        have := Torus.continuous_mode k
1100        fun_prop) (B := 1) (fun k y => by
1101          refine (Complex.abs_re_le_norm _).trans ?_
1102          rw [norm_mul, Complex.norm_conj, norm_mode, norm_mode]; simp)]
1103    refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
1104    congr 1
1105    have := integral_re (μ := Q) (f := fun y => Torus.mode k x * conj (Torus.mode k y)) 
1106      (((Torus.continuous_mode k).comp continuous_id |>.star.const_mul _).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
1107        (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _))
1108    simp only [RCLike.re_to_complex] at this
1109    rw [this, integral_const_mul, integral_conj]
1110  simp_rw [hinner]
1111  rw [integral_tsum_c hc P (fun k x => (Torus.mode k x * conj (∫ y, Torus.mode k y ∂Q)).re)
1112    (fun k => by
1113      have := Torus.continuous_mode k
1114      fun_prop) (B := (Q Set.univ).toReal) (fun k x => by
1115        refine (Complex.abs_re_le_norm _).trans ?_
1116        rw [norm_mul, Complex.norm_conj, norm_mode, one_mul]
1117        refine (norm_integral_le_integral_norm _).trans ?_
1118        simp [norm_mode, Measure.real])]
1119  refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
1120  congr 1
1121  have := integral_re (μ := P) (f := fun x => Torus.mode k x * conj (∫ y, Torus.mode k y ∂Q))
1122    (((Torus.continuous_mode k).mul continuous_const).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
1123        (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _))
1124  simp only [RCLike.re_to_complex] at this
1125  rw [this, integral_mul_const]

1130theorem integral_empirical_mode {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (X : Config d N) (k : Lattice d) :
1131    ∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂(empirical X) = ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) • ∑ i, Torus.mode k (X i) := by
1132  unfold empirical
1133  rw [integral_smul_measure, integral_finsetSum_measure]
1134  · simp [integral_dirac, ENNReal.toReal_inv]
1135  · intro i _
1136    exact integrable_dirac (by simp)

1138theorem integral_empirical_real {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (X : Config d N) (φ : Torus d → ℝ) :
1139    ∫ x, φ x ∂(empirical X) = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ i, φ (X i) := pair_empirical hN X φ

1141theorem Q_eq_tsum {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) {c : Lattice d → ℝ} (hc : Summable fun k => |c k|)
1142    (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] :
1143    (1 / (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j, trigSum c (X i - X j)
1144      - (2 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i, ∫ y, trigSum c (X i - y) ∂μ
1145      + ∫ x, ∫ y, trigSum c (x - y) ∂μ ∂μ =
1146    ∑' k, c k * ‖((N : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) • ∑ i, Torus.mode k (X i) - ∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂μ‖ ^ 2 := by
1147  haveI := empirical_isProbabilityMeasure hN X
1148  set ν := empirical X with hν
1149  have hnu : ∀ k, ∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂ν = ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) • ∑ i, Torus.mode k (X i) :=
1150    integral_empirical_mode hN X
1151  have hN0 : (N : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hN.ne'
1152  -- the three bilinear terms
1153  have hνν := integral_trigSum_sub hc ν ν
1154  have hνμ := integral_trigSum_sub hc ν μ
1155  have hμμ := integral_trigSum_sub hc μ μ
1156  have e1 : ∫ x, ∫ y, trigSum c (x - y) ∂ν ∂ν =
1157      (1 / (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j, trigSum c (X i - X j) := by
1158    rw [integral_empirical_real hN X]
1159    have : ∀ i, ∫ y, trigSum c (X i - y) ∂ν = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ j, trigSum c (X i - X j) := fun i =>
1160      integral_empirical_real hN X (fun y => trigSum c (X i - y))
1161    simp_rw [this]
1162    rw [← Finset.mul_sum, ← mul_assoc]
1163    congr 1
1164    field_simp
1165  have e2 : ∫ x, ∫ y, trigSum c (x - y) ∂μ ∂ν =
1166      (1 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i, ∫ y, trigSum c (X i - y) ∂μ := by
1167    rw [integral_empirical_real hN X]
1168    simp
1169  have e3 : (2 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i, ∫ y, trigSum c (X i - y) ∂μ =
1170      2 * ((1 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i, ∫ y, trigSum c (X i - y) ∂μ) := by ring
1171  rw [← e1, e3, ← e2, hνν, hνμ, hμμ]
1172  -- pointwise algebra and summability
1173  set z : Lattice d → ℂ := fun k => ∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂ν with hz
1174  set w : Lattice d → ℂ := fun k => ∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂μ with hw
1175  have hzn : ∀ k, ‖z k‖ ≤ 1 := fun k => by
1176    refine (norm_integral_le_integral_norm _).trans ?_
1177    simp [norm_mode]
1178  have hwn : ∀ k, ‖w k‖ ≤ 1 := fun k => by
1179    refine (norm_integral_le_integral_norm _).trans ?_
1180    simp [norm_mode]
1181  have hbd : ∀ (p q : ℂ), ‖p‖ ≤ 1 → ‖q‖ ≤ 1 → |(p * (starRingEnd ℂ) q).re| ≤ 1 := by
1182    intro p q hp hq
1183    refine (Complex.abs_re_le_norm _).trans ?_
1184    rw [norm_mul, Complex.norm_conj]
1185    nlinarith [norm_nonneg p, norm_nonneg q]
1186  have hs : ∀ p q : Lattice d → ℂ, (∀ k, ‖p k‖ ≤ 1) → (∀ k, ‖q k‖ ≤ 1) →
1187      Summable fun k => c k * (p k * (starRingEnd ℂ) (q k)).re := by
1188    intro p q hp hq
1189    refine Summable.of_norm_bounded hc fun k => ?_
1190    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul]
1191    calc |c k| * |(p k * (starRingEnd ℂ) (q k)).re| ≤ |c k| * 1 :=
1192          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hbd _ _ (hp k) (hq k)) (abs_nonneg _)
1193      _ = _ := mul_one _
1194  have h1 := hs z z hzn hzn
1195  have h2 := hs z w hzn hwn
1196  have h3 := hs w w hwn hwn
1197  have hpt : ∀ p q : ℂ, ‖p - q‖ ^ 2 = (p * (starRingEnd ℂ) p).re - 2 * (p * (starRingEnd ℂ) q).re
1198      + (q * (starRingEnd ℂ) q).re := by
1199    intro p q
1200    rw [Complex.sq_norm, Complex.normSq_apply]
1201    simp only [Complex.mul_re, Complex.conj_re, Complex.conj_im, Complex.sub_re, Complex.sub_im]
1202    ring
1203  have hz' : ∀ k, ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) • ∑ i, Torus.mode k (X i) = z k := fun k => (hnu k).symm
1204  simp_rw [hz']
1205  have hrw : ∀ k, c k * ‖z k - ∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂μ‖ ^ 2 =
1206      c k * (z k * (starRingEnd ℂ) (z k)).re - 2 * (c k * (z k * (starRingEnd ℂ) (w k)).re) +
1207        c k * (w k * (starRingEnd ℂ) (w k)).re := by
1208    intro k
1209    rw [hpt]
1210    simp only [hw]
1211    ring
1212  simp_rw [hrw]
1213  rw [Summable.tsum_add (h1.sub (h2.mul_left 2)) h3, Summable.tsum_sub h1 (h2.mul_left 2), tsum_mul_left]

1224theorem measurable_periodicRiesz_one (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
1225    Measurable (periodicRiesz d s) := by
1226  let εn : ℕ → ℝ := fun n => 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1)
1227  have hεn : Tendsto εn atTop (𝓝[>] 0) := by
1228    refine tendsto_nhdsWithin_iff.mpr ⟨tendsto_one_div_add_atTop_nhds_zero_nat, ?_⟩
1229    exact Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun n => by simp only [εn, Set.mem_Ioi]; positivity
1230  let f : ℕ → Torus d → ℝ := fun n x => if x = 0 then 0 else heatRiesz d s (εn n) x
1231  have hf : ∀ n, Measurable (f n) := fun n =>
1232    Measurable.ite (measurableSet_singleton (0 : Torus d)) measurable_const
1233      (_root_.HRS.continuous_heatRiesz hsd (εn n) (by simp only [εn]; positivity)).measurable
1234  let g' : Torus d → ℝ := fun x => if x = 0 then 0 else periodicRiesz d s x
1235  have hg' : Measurable g' := by
1236    refine measurable_of_tendsto_metrizable hf (tendsto_pi_nhds.mpr fun x => ?_)
1237    by_cases hx : x = 0
1238    · simp [f, g', hx]
1239    · simp only [f, g', hx, if_false]
1240      exact (periodicRiesz_tendsto_one hd hs0 hsd x hx).comp hεn
1241  have : periodicRiesz d s = fun x => if x = 0 then periodicRiesz d s 0 else g' x := by
1242    funext x
1243    by_cases hx : x = 0
1244    · subst hx; simp
1245    · simp [g', hx]
1246  rw [this]
1247  exact Measurable.ite (measurableSet_singleton (0 : Torus d)) measurable_const hg'

1249/-- the near part `f_ε = g - g_ε` (with the value `0` at the origin). -/
1250def nearF (d : ℕ) (s ε : ℝ) (z : Torus d) : ℝ :=
1251  if z = 0 then 0 else periodicRiesz d s z - gT d s ε z
1252
1253/-- the lower bound `B' = A ε^a / a` of the near part. -/
1254def nearB (d : ℕ) (s ε : ℝ) : ℝ :=
1255  subA d s * (ε ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2))
1256
1257theorem nearB_nonneg (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
1258    0 ≤ nearB d s ε := by
1259  have ha : 0 < ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 := by linarith
1260  have := subA_nonneg_one hd hs0 hsd
1261  unfold nearB
1262  positivity
1263
1264theorem nearF_ge (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (z : Torus d) :
1265    -nearB d s ε ≤ nearF d s ε z := by
1266  unfold nearF
1267  by_cases hz : z = 0
1268  · simp only [hz, ite_true]
1269    have := nearB_nonneg hd hs0 hsd hε
1270    linarith
1271  · simp only [hz, ite_false]
1272    exact near_ge hd hs0 hsd hε z hz
1273
1274theorem periodicRiesz_eq_add {ε : ℝ} (z : Torus d) (hz : z ≠ 0) :
1275    periodicRiesz d s z = gT d s ε z + nearF d s ε z := by
1276  simp [nearF, hz]
1277
1278theorem measurable_nearF (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
1279    Measurable (nearF d s ε) := by
1280  unfold nearF
1281  exact Measurable.ite (measurableSet_singleton (0 : Torus d)) measurable_const
1282    ((measurable_periodicRiesz_one hd hs0 hsd).sub (continuous_gT hd hs0 hsd hε).measurable)
1283
1284theorem integral_cosMode_of_ne (k : Lattice d) (hk : k ≠ 0) : ∫ x, Torus.cosMode k x = 0 := by
1285  have h := Torus.integral_mode k
1286  simp only [hk, if_false] at h
1287  unfold Torus.cosMode
1288  have hint : Integrable (Torus.mode k) (volume : Measure (Torus d)) :=
1289    (Torus.continuous_mode k).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
1290  have := integral_re (μ := (volume : Measure (Torus d))) hint
1291  simp only [RCLike.re_to_complex] at this
1292  rw [this, h]; simp

1294theorem integral_gT (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
1295    ∫ x, gT d s ε x = 0 := by
1296  have hc : Summable fun k => |cT d s ε k| := (summable_cT hd hs0 hsd hε).abs
1297  unfold gT
1298  rw [integral_tsum_c hc (volume : Measure (Torus d)) (fun k x => Torus.cosMode k x)
1299    (fun k => Torus.continuous_cosMode k) (B := 1) (fun k x => abs_cosMode_le k x)]
1300  have : ∀ k, cT d s ε k * ∫ x, Torus.cosMode k x = 0 := by
1301    intro k; by_cases hk : k = 0
1302    · simp [cT, hk]
1303    · rw [integral_cosMode_of_ne k hk, mul_zero]
1304  simp_rw [this]; exact tsum_zero
1305
1306theorem integrable_nearF (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
1307    Integrable (nearF d s ε) := by
1308  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
1309  have h1 := (integrable_periodicRiesz_one hd hs0 hsd).sub
1310    ((continuous_gT hd hs0 hsd hε).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _))
1311  refine h1.congr ?_
1312  filter_upwards [torus_ae_ne hd0] with z hz
1313  simp [nearF, hz]
1314
1315theorem integral_nearF (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
1316    ∫ z, nearF d s ε z = 0 := by
1317  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
1318  have h1 : ∫ z, nearF d s ε z = ∫ z, (periodicRiesz d s z - gT d s ε z) := by
1319    refine integral_congr_ae ?_
1320    filter_upwards [torus_ae_ne hd0] with z hz
1321    simp [nearF, hz]
1322  rw [h1, integral_sub (integrable_periodicRiesz_one hd hs0 hsd)
1323    ((continuous_gT hd hs0 hsd hε).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)),
1324    (integral_periodicRiesz_one hd hs0 hsd).2, integral_gT hd hs0 hsd hε]
1325  simp
1326
1327end CoercNear
1328
1329

1332section CoercConv
1333
1334open Set KernelAux2
1335
1336variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}
1337
1338theorem integrable_conv_density {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
1339    {φ : Torus d → ℝ} (hφm : Measurable φ) (hφ : Integrable φ) (x : Torus d) :
1340    Integrable (fun y => φ (x - y)) (withDensityReal ρ) := by
1341  refine ⟨(hφm.comp (measurable_const.sub measurable_id)).aestronglyMeasurable, ?_⟩
1342  have h1 := lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ hΛ (fun z => ‖φ z‖ₑ) x
1343  have h2 := hφ.hasFiniteIntegral
1344  unfold HasFiniteIntegral at h2 ⊢
1345  exact lt_of_le_of_lt h1 (ENNReal.mul_lt_top ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top h2)
1346
1347theorem integral_conv_density_le {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
1348    {φ : Torus d → ℝ} (hφ0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ φ z) (hφm : Measurable φ) (hφ : Integrable φ) (x : Torus d) :
1349    ∫ y, φ (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ Λ * ∫ y, φ y := by
1350  have hint := integrable_conv_density hΛ hφm hφ x
1351  rw [integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => hφ0 (x - y))
1352    (hφm.comp (measurable_const.sub measurable_id)).aestronglyMeasurable,
1353    integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hφ0) hφm.aestronglyMeasurable]
1354  have h1 := lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ hΛ (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (φ z)) x
1355  have h2 : ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (φ y) < ⊤ := hφ.lintegral_lt_top
1356  calc (∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (φ (x - y)) ∂(withDensityReal ρ)).toReal
1357      ≤ (ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (φ y)).toReal :=
1358        ENNReal.toReal_mono (ENNReal.mul_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top h2.ne) h1
1359    _ = Λ * (∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (φ y)).toReal := by
1360        rw [ENNReal.toReal_mul, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hΛ0]
1361
1362end CoercConv
1363

1367section CoercEnergy
1368
1369open Set KernelAux
1370
1371variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}
1372
1373theorem nearF_conv_bounds (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε)
1374    {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
1375    (x : Torus d) :
1376    Integrable (fun y => nearF d s ε (x - y)) (withDensityReal ρ) ∧
1377    -nearB d s ε ≤ ∫ y, nearF d s ε (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ∧
1378    ∫ y, nearF d s ε (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ Λ * nearB d s ε := by
1379  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
1380  have hm := measurable_nearF hd hs0 hsd hε
1381  have hi := integrable_nearF hd hs0 hsd hε
1382  have hB := nearB_nonneg hd hs0 hsd hε
1383  have hint := integrable_conv_density hΛ hm hi x
1384  refine ⟨hint, ?_, ?_⟩
1385  · have := integral_mono (integrable_const (-nearB d s ε)) hint
1386      (fun y => nearF_ge hd hs0 hsd hε (x - y))
1387    simpa using this
1388  · set H : Torus d → ℝ := fun z => nearF d s ε z + nearB d s ε with hH
1389    have hH0 : ∀ z, 0 ≤ H z := fun z => by
1390      have := nearF_ge hd hs0 hsd hε z
1391      simp only [hH]; linarith
1392    have hHm : Measurable H := hm.add measurable_const
1393    have hHi : Integrable H := hi.add (integrable_const _)
1394    have h1 := integral_conv_density_le hΛ0 hΛ hH0 hHm hHi x
1395    have h2 : ∫ y, H y = nearB d s ε := by
1396      simp only [hH]
1397      rw [integral_add hi (integrable_const _), integral_nearF hd hs0 hsd hε]
1398      simp
1399    have h3 : ∫ y, H (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) =
1400        ∫ y, nearF d s ε (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) + nearB d s ε := by
1401      simp only [hH]
1402      rw [integral_add hint (integrable_const _)]
1403      simp
1404    rw [h2, h3] at h1
1405    nlinarith
1406
1407theorem J_bounds (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε)
1408    {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
1409    (x : Torus d) :
1410    Integrable (fun y => periodicRiesz d s (x - y)) (withDensityReal ρ) ∧
1411    ∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) - nearB d s ε ≤
1412      ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ∧
1413    ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤
1414      ∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) + Λ * nearB d s ε := by
1415  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
1416  obtain ⟨hint, hlo, hhi⟩ := nearF_conv_bounds hd hs0 hsd hε hρ hΛ0 hΛ x
1417  have hG : Integrable (fun y => gT d s ε (x - y)) (withDensityReal ρ) :=
1418    ((continuous_gT hd hs0 hsd hε).comp (continuous_const.sub continuous_id)).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
1419      (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
1420  have hae : ∀ᵐ y ∂(withDensityReal ρ), periodicRiesz d s (x - y) =
1421      gT d s ε (x - y) + nearF d s ε (x - y) := by
1422    have h0 : ∀ᵐ y ∂(volume : Measure (Torus d)), y ≠ x := by
1423      rw [ae_iff]
1424      have := volume_singleton_torus hd x
1425      simpa using this
1426    have h1 : ∀ᵐ y ∂(withDensityReal ρ), y ≠ x := withDensity_absolutelyContinuous _ _ h0
1427    filter_upwards [h1] with y hy
1428    exact periodicRiesz_eq_add (x - y) (sub_ne_zero.mpr (Ne.symm hy))
1429  have hI : Integrable (fun y => periodicRiesz d s (x - y)) (withDensityReal ρ) :=
1430    (hG.add hint).congr (hae.mono fun y hy => hy.symm)
1431  have heq : ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) =
1432      ∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) + ∫ y, nearF d s ε (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) := by
1433    rw [integral_congr_ae hae, integral_add hG hint]
1434  refine ⟨hI, ?_, ?_⟩ <;> rw [heq] <;> linarith
1435
1436end CoercEnergy
1437
