1441section CoercEnergy2
1442
1443open Set KernelAux
1444
1445variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}
1446
1447theorem rieszEnergy_ge_aux (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε)
1448    {ρ : Torus d → ℝ} (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) {Λ : ℝ} (hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
1449    {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (X : Config d N) (hX : X ∉ collisionSet d N) :
1450    1 / 2 * ((1 / (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j, gT d s ε (X i - X j)
1451        - (2 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i, ∫ y, gT d s ε (X i - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ)
1452        + ∫ x, ∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ∂(withDensityReal ρ))
1453      - gT d s ε 0 / (2 * N) - (1 + Λ) * nearB d s ε ≤ rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) := by
1454  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
1455  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
1456  have hB := nearB_nonneg hd hs0 hsd hε
1457  have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1458  have hdist : ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j → X i - X j ≠ 0 := by
1459    intro i j hij h
1460    exact hX ⟨i, j, hij, sub_eq_zero.mp h⟩
1461  -- the pair term
1462  have hT1 : ∀ i : Fin N, ∑ j, gT d s ε (X i - X j) - gT d s ε 0 - N * nearB d s ε ≤
1463      ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, periodicRiesz d s (X i - X j) := by
1464    intro i
1465    have h1 : ∀ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, gT d s ε (X i - X j) - nearB d s ε ≤
1466        periodicRiesz d s (X i - X j) := by
1467      intro j hj
1468      have hij : i ≠ j := fun h => (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hj) h.symm
1469      rw [periodicRiesz_eq_add (ε := ε) (X i - X j) (hdist i j hij)]
1470      have := nearF_ge hd hs0 hsd hε (X i - X j)
1471      linarith
1472    have h2 := Finset.sum_le_sum h1
1473    rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib] at h2
1474    have h3 : ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, gT d s ε (X i - X j) =
1475        ∑ j, gT d s ε (X i - X j) - gT d s ε 0 := by
1476      rw [← Finset.add_sum_erase _ _ (Finset.mem_univ i)]
1477      simp
1478    have h4 : ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, nearB d s ε = ((N : ℝ) - 1) * nearB d s ε := by
1479      rw [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_erase_of_mem (Finset.mem_univ i), Finset.card_univ,
1480        Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
1481      rw [Nat.cast_sub hN]; simp
1482    rw [h3, h4] at h2
1483    nlinarith
1484  -- the cross term
1485  have hJ := fun x => J_bounds hd hs0 hsd hε hρ hΛ0 hΛ x
1486  have hT2 : ∑ i, ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (X i - y) ∂μ ≤
1487      ∑ i, ∫ y, gT d s ε (X i - y) ∂μ + N * (Λ * nearB d s ε) := by
1488    have := Finset.sum_le_sum (fun i (_ : i ∈ Finset.univ) => (hJ (X i)).2.2)
1489    rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib] at this
1490    simpa using this
1491  -- the double integral term
1492  have hGc : Continuous (gT d s ε) := continuous_gT hd hs0 hsd hε
1493  have hIc : Continuous fun x => ∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂μ := continuous_conv0 hGc μ
1494  have hIint : Integrable (fun x => ∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂μ) μ :=
1495    hIc.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
1496  have hJm : StronglyMeasurable fun x => ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (x - y) ∂μ :=
1497    StronglyMeasurable.integral_prod_right'
1498      (f := fun p : Torus d × Torus d => periodicRiesz d s (p.1 - p.2))
1499      ((measurable_periodicRiesz_one hd hs0 hsd).comp (measurable_fst.sub measurable_snd)).stronglyMeasurable
1500  have hJint : Integrable (fun x => ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (x - y) ∂μ) μ := by
1501    refine Integrable.mono' (hIint.norm.add (integrable_const ((1 + Λ) * nearB d s ε)))
1502      hJm.aestronglyMeasurable (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
1503    obtain ⟨-, h1, h2⟩ := hJ x
1504    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_le]
1505    simp only [Pi.add_apply, Real.norm_eq_abs]
1506    have := neg_abs_le (∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂μ)
1507    have := le_abs_self (∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂μ)
1508    have : 0 ≤ Λ * nearB d s ε := mul_nonneg hΛ0 hB
1509    constructor <;> nlinarith
1510  have hT3 : ∫ x, ∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂μ ∂μ - nearB d s ε ≤
1511      ∫ x, ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (x - y) ∂μ ∂μ := by
1512    have := integral_mono (hIint.sub (integrable_const (nearB d s ε))) hJint
1513      (fun x => (hJ x).2.1)
1514    simp only [Pi.sub_apply] at this
1515    rw [integral_sub hIint (integrable_const _)] at this
1516    simpa using this
1517  -- assemble
1518  unfold rieszEnergy modulatedEnergyKernel modulatedEnergy
1519  have hsum1 : ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, periodicRiesz d s (X i - X j) ≥
1520      ∑ i, ∑ j, gT d s ε (X i - X j) - N * gT d s ε 0 - N * (N * nearB d s ε) := by
1521    have := Finset.sum_le_sum (fun i (_ : i ∈ Finset.univ) => hT1 i)
1522    rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, Finset.sum_sub_distrib] at this
1523    simpa using this
1524  have hN2 : (0 : ℝ) < (N : ℝ) ^ 2 := by positivity
1525  show 1 / 2 * (1 / (N : ℝ) ^ 2 * ∑ i, ∑ j, gT d s ε (X i - X j) -
1526        2 / (N : ℝ) * ∑ i, ∫ y, gT d s ε (X i - y) ∂μ +
1527        ∫ x, ∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂μ ∂μ) - gT d s ε 0 / (2 * N) - (1 + Λ) * nearB d s ε ≤
1528      1 / (2 * (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, periodicRiesz d s (X i - X j) -
1529        1 / (N : ℝ) * ∑ i, ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (X i - y) ∂μ +
1530        1 / 2 * ∫ x, ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (x - y) ∂μ ∂μ
1531  set SG := ∑ i, ∑ j, gT d s ε (X i - X j)
1532  set I1 := ∑ i, ∫ y, gT d s ε (X i - y) ∂μ
1533  set D := ∫ x, ∫ y, gT d s ε (x - y) ∂μ ∂μ
1534  set G0 := gT d s ε 0
1535  set B := nearB d s ε
1536  have a1 : 1 / (2 * (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * (SG - N * G0 - N * (N * B)) ≤
1537      1 / (2 * (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, periodicRiesz d s (X i - X j) :=
1538    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hsum1 (by positivity)
1539  have a2 : 1 / (N : ℝ) * ∑ i, ∫ y, periodicRiesz d s (X i - y) ∂μ ≤
1540      1 / (N : ℝ) * (I1 + N * (Λ * B)) :=
1541    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hT2 (by positivity)
1542  have key : 1 / (2 * (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * (SG - N * G0 - N * (N * B)) -
1543      1 / (N : ℝ) * (I1 + N * (Λ * B)) + 1 / 2 * (D - B) =
1544      1 / 2 * (1 / (N : ℝ) ^ 2 * SG - 2 / (N : ℝ) * I1 + D) - G0 / (2 * N) - (1 + Λ) * B := by
1545    field_simp
1546    ring
1547  nlinarith [hT3]
1548
1549end CoercEnergy2
1550

1554section CoercScale
1555
1556variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}
1557
1558theorem smearScale_pos {N : ℕ} {Λ : ℝ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) (hΛ : 0 < Λ) : 0 < smearScale d N Λ := by
1559  unfold smearScale
1560  have : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1561  exact Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by positivity) _
1562
1563theorem smearScale_lt_one (hd : 1 ≤ d) {N : ℕ} {Λ : ℝ} (hNΛ : 1 < (N : ℝ) * Λ) :
1564    smearScale d N Λ < 1 := by
1565  unfold smearScale
1566  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
1567  have hdne : (d : ℝ) ≠ 0 := hd0.ne'
1568  exact Real.rpow_lt_one_of_one_lt_of_neg hNΛ (by
1569    have : (0:ℝ) < 1 / d := by positivity
1570    have e : -(1 : ℝ) / d = -(1 / d) := by ring
1571    rw [e]; linarith)
1572
1573theorem smearScale_sq_rpow (hd : 1 ≤ d) {N : ℕ} {Λ : ℝ} (hq : 0 < (N : ℝ) * Λ) (r : ℝ) :
1574    (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) ^ r = ((N : ℝ) * Λ) ^ (-2 * r / d) := by
1575  unfold smearScale
1576  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
1577  have hdne : (d : ℝ) ≠ 0 := hd0.ne'
1578  rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hq.le, ← Real.rpow_mul hq.le]
1579  congr 1
1580  push_cast
1581  field_simp
1582
1583theorem smear_a_eq (hd : 1 ≤ d) {N : ℕ} {Λ : ℝ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) (hΛ : 0 < Λ) :
1584    Λ * (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) = Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := by
1585  have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1586  have hq : 0 < (N : ℝ) * Λ := by positivity
1587  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
1588  have hdne : (d : ℝ) ≠ 0 := hd0.ne'
1589  rw [smearScale_sq_rpow hd hq]
1590  have e : -2 * (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) / d = s / d - 1 := by field_simp; ring
1591  rw [e, Real.mul_rpow hN0.le hΛ.le]
1592  have : Λ * Λ ^ (s / d - 1) = Λ ^ (s / d) := by
1593    calc Λ * Λ ^ (s / d - 1) = Λ ^ (1 : ℝ) * Λ ^ (s / d - 1) := by rw [Real.rpow_one]
1594      _ = Λ ^ (1 + (s / d - 1)) := (Real.rpow_add hΛ _ _).symm
1595      _ = _ := by congr 1; ring
1596  calc Λ * ((N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) * Λ ^ (s / d - 1)) = (Λ * Λ ^ (s / d - 1)) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := by ring
1597    _ = _ := by rw [this]
1598
1599theorem smear_neg_eq (hd : 1 ≤ d) {N : ℕ} {Λ : ℝ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) (hΛ : 0 < Λ) :
1600    (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) ^ (-s / 2) / (N : ℝ) = Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := by
1601  have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1602  have hq : 0 < (N : ℝ) * Λ := by positivity
1603  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
1604  have hdne : (d : ℝ) ≠ 0 := hd0.ne'
1605  rw [smearScale_sq_rpow hd hq]
1606  have e : -2 * (-s / 2) / d = s / d := by field_simp
1607  rw [e, Real.mul_rpow hN0.le hΛ.le, Real.rpow_sub hN0, Real.rpow_one]
1608  field_simp
1609
1610theorem smear_log_eq (hd : 1 ≤ d) {N : ℕ} {Λ : ℝ} (hN : 1 ≤ N) (hΛ : 0 < Λ) :
1611    Real.log (1 / smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) = 2 / d * Real.log ((N : ℝ) * Λ) := by
1612  have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1613  have hq : 0 < (N : ℝ) * Λ := by positivity
1614  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
1615  have hdne : (d : ℝ) ≠ 0 := hd0.ne'
1616  have hl := smearScale_pos (d := d) hN hΛ
1617  unfold smearScale
1618  rw [one_div, Real.log_inv, Real.log_pow, Real.log_rpow hq]
1619  push_cast
1620  field_simp
1621
1622end CoercScale
1623
1624
1625
1626section CoercConst
1627
1628open Set KernelAux
1629
1630variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}
1631
1632theorem diag_const (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
1633    ∃ C₀ : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₀ ∧ ∀ Λ : ℝ, 1 ≤ Λ → ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N →
1634      gT d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) 0 / (2 * N) + (1 + Λ) * nearB d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) ≤
1635        oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N := by
1636  have hd0 : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast hd
1637  have ha : 0 < ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 := by linarith
1638  have hA := subA_nonneg_one hd hs0 hsd
1639  -- the `nearB` part
1640  have hnear : ∀ Λ : ℝ, 1 ≤ Λ → ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N →
1641      (1 + Λ) * nearB d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) ≤
1642        (2 * subA d s / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2)) * (Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)) := by
1643    intro Λ hΛ N hN
1644    have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by omega
1645    have hΛ0 : 0 < Λ := by linarith
1646    have hB := nearB_nonneg hd hs0 hsd (ε := smearScale d N Λ ^ 2)
1647      (by have := smearScale_pos (d := d) hN1 hΛ0; positivity)
1648    rw [← smear_a_eq (s := s) hd hN1 hΛ0]
1649    unfold nearB at hB ⊢
1650    have : (1 + Λ) * (subA d s * ((smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2))) ≤
1651        (2 * Λ) * (subA d s * ((smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) ^ (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2))) :=
1652      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) hB
1653    refine this.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
1654    field_simp
1655  have hq : ∀ Λ : ℝ, 1 ≤ Λ → ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N →
1656      0 < smearScale d N Λ ^ 2 ∧ smearScale d N Λ ^ 2 ≤ 1 := by
1657    intro Λ hΛ N hN
1658    have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by omega
1659    have hΛ0 : 0 < Λ := by linarith
1660    have hNΛ : 1 < (N : ℝ) * Λ := by
1661      have : (2 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
1662      nlinarith
1663    have h1 := smearScale_pos (d := d) hN1 hΛ0
1664    have h2 := smearScale_lt_one hd hNΛ
1665    exact ⟨by positivity, by nlinarith⟩
1666  by_cases hs : s = 0
1667  · subst hs
1668    obtain ⟨C, hC⟩ := gT_zero_le_zero (d := d) hd hd0
1669    refine ⟨max C 0 / 2 + 2 * subA d 0 / (((d : ℝ) - 0) / 2), by positivity, fun Λ hΛ N hN => ?_⟩
1670    have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by omega
1671    have hΛ0 : 0 < Λ := by linarith
1672    obtain ⟨hq0, hq1⟩ := hq Λ hΛ N hN
1673    have h1 := hC _ hq0 hq1
1674    rw [smear_log_eq hd hN1 hΛ0] at h1
1675    have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN1
1676    have h2 := hnear Λ hΛ N hN
1677    have hone : Λ ^ ((0 : ℝ) / d) = 1 := by rw [zero_div, Real.rpow_zero]
1678    have e : (N : ℝ) ^ ((0 : ℝ) / d - 1) = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by
1679      rw [zero_div, zero_sub, Real.rpow_neg_one]
1680    rw [hone, e] at h2
1681    have h3 : gT d 0 (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) 0 / (2 * N) ≤
1682        Real.log (N * Λ) / (2 * d * N) + max C 0 / 2 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by
1683      rw [div_le_iff₀ (by positivity)]
1684      have : Real.log (N * Λ) / (2 * d * N) * (2 * N) = 1 / d * Real.log (N * Λ) := by
1685        field_simp
1686      have h4 : C ≤ max C 0 := le_max_left _ _
1687      have h5 : max C 0 / 2 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * (2 * N) = max C 0 := by field_simp
1688      calc gT d 0 (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) 0 ≤ 1 / 2 * (2 / d * Real.log (N * Λ)) + C := h1
1689        _ ≤ 1 / d * Real.log (N * Λ) + max C 0 := by
1690            have : 1 / 2 * (2 / (d : ℝ) * Real.log (N * Λ)) = 1 / d * Real.log (N * Λ) := by ring
1691            rw [this]; linarith
1692        _ = _ := by rw [add_mul, this, h5]
1693    unfold oNΛ
1694    rw [if_pos rfl, hone, e]
1695    have h6 : (max C 0 / 2 + 2 * subA d 0 / (((d : ℝ) - 0) / 2)) * 1 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ =
1696        max C 0 / 2 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ + 2 * subA d 0 / (((d : ℝ) - 0) / 2) * (1 * (N : ℝ)⁻¹) := by ring
1697    rw [h6]
1698    linarith
1699  · have hspos : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm hs)
1700    obtain ⟨C, hC0, hC⟩ := gT_zero_le_pos hd hspos hsd
1701    refine ⟨C / 2 + 2 * subA d s / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2), by positivity, fun Λ hΛ N hN => ?_⟩
1702    have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by omega
1703    have hΛ0 : 0 < Λ := by linarith
1704    obtain ⟨hq0, hq1⟩ := hq Λ hΛ N hN
1705    have h1 := hC _ hq0 hq1
1706    have hNpos : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast hN1
1707    have h2 := hnear Λ hΛ N hN
1708    have h3 : gT d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) 0 / (2 * N) ≤
1709        C / 2 * (Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)) := by
1710      rw [div_le_iff₀ (by positivity)]
1711      have h5 := smear_neg_eq (s := s) hd hN1 hΛ0
1712      have h6 : (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) ^ (-s / 2) = (Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)) * N := by
1713        rw [← h5]; field_simp
1714      calc gT d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) 0 ≤ C * (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) ^ (-s / 2) := h1
1715        _ = C / 2 * (Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)) * (2 * N) := by rw [h6]; ring
1716    unfold oNΛ
1717    rw [if_neg hs, zero_add]
1718    have h7 : (C / 2 + 2 * subA d s / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2)) * Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) =
1719        C / 2 * (Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)) +
1720          2 * subA d s / (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) * (Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1)) := by ring
1721    rw [h7]
1722    linarith
1723
1724end CoercConst
1725

1727section CoercMain
1728
1729open Set KernelAux
1730
1731variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}
1732
1733/-- the Fourier form `E_ε(ρ) = ∑_k ĝ_ε(k) |ρ̂(k)|²` of the signed measure `μ_N - μ`. -/
1734def Esq (d : ℕ) (s ε : ℝ) {N : ℕ} (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) : ℝ :=
1735  ∑' k : Lattice d, cT d s ε k *
1736    ‖((N : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) • ∑ i, Torus.mode k (X i) - ∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂μ‖ ^ 2
1737
1738theorem Esq_nonneg (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) {N : ℕ}
1739    (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) : 0 ≤ Esq d s ε X μ :=
1740  tsum_nonneg fun k => mul_nonneg (cT_nonneg hd hs0 hsd hε k) (sq_nonneg _)
1741
1742theorem coercivity_energy (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
1743    ∃ C₀ : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₀ ∧ ∀ Λ : ℝ, 1 ≤ Λ → ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N →
1744      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
1745      ∀ X : Config d N, X ∉ collisionSet d N →
1746        0 ≤ Esq d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) X (withDensityReal ρ) ∧
1747        Esq d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) X (withDensityReal ρ) ≤
1748          2 * (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N) := by
1749  obtain ⟨C₀, hC₀, hconst⟩ := diag_const hd hs0 hsd
1750  refine ⟨C₀, hC₀, fun Λ hΛ N hN ρ hρ hρΛ X hX => ?_⟩
1751  have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by omega
1752  have hΛ0 : 0 < Λ := by linarith
1753  have hε : 0 < smearScale d N Λ ^ 2 := by
1754    have := smearScale_pos (d := d) hN1 hΛ0; positivity
1755  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
1756  have hQ := Q_eq_tsum (c := cT d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2)) (by omega) 
1757    ((summable_cT hd hs0 hsd hε).abs) X (withDensityReal ρ)
1758  have hge := rieszEnergy_ge_aux hd hs0 hsd hε hρ hΛ0.le hρΛ (by omega) X hX
1759  have hc := hconst Λ hΛ N hN
1760  have hQ' : Esq d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) X (withDensityReal ρ) =
1761      (1 / (N : ℝ) ^ 2) * ∑ i, ∑ j, gT d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) (X i - X j)
1762        - (2 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i, ∫ y, gT d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) (X i - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ)
1763        + ∫ x, ∫ y, gT d s (smearScale d N Λ ^ 2) (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) :=
1764    hQ.symm
1765  refine ⟨Esq_nonneg hd hs0 hsd hε X _, ?_⟩
1766  rw [hQ']
1767  linarith
1768
1769end CoercMain
1770

1772section CoercHeat
1773
1774open Set KernelAux
1775
1776variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}
1777
1778/-- the periodic heat kernel `p_t(y) = ∑_k e^{-t|k|²} cos(2π k·y)`. -/
1779def heatK (d : ℕ) (t : ℝ) (y : Torus d) : ℝ :=
1780  ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k y
1781
1782theorem summable_heatK_coef {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) :
1783    Summable fun k : Lattice d => |Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)| := by
1784  refine (summable_gauss_lattice d t ht).congr fun k => ?_
1785  rw [abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1786
1787theorem continuous_heatK {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) : Continuous (heatK d t) := by
1788  unfold heatK
1789  refine continuous_tsum (u := fun k : Lattice d => Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2))
1790    (fun k => continuous_const.mul (Torus.continuous_cosMode k)) (summable_gauss_lattice d t ht)
1791    (fun k x => ?_)
1792  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1793  calc Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * |Torus.cosMode k x|
1794      ≤ Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 1 :=
1795        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (abs_cosMode_le k x) (Real.exp_pos _).le
1796    _ = _ := mul_one _
1797
1798theorem heatK_nonneg {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) (y : Torus d) : 0 ≤ heatK d t y :=
1799  theta_nonneg y ht
1800
1801theorem integral_heatK {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) : ∫ y, heatK d t y = 1 := by
1802  unfold heatK
1803  rw [integral_tsum_c (summable_heatK_coef ht) (volume : Measure (Torus d))
1804    (fun k x => Torus.cosMode k x) (fun k => Torus.continuous_cosMode k) (B := 1)
1805    (fun k x => abs_cosMode_le k x)]
1806  have : ∀ k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * ∫ x, Torus.cosMode k x =
1807      if k = 0 then 1 else 0 := by
1808    intro k
1809    by_cases hk : k = 0
1810    · subst hk
1811      have h1 : ∫ x : Torus d, Torus.cosMode (0 : Lattice d) x = 1 := by simp [cosMode_zero]
1812      have h0 : Lattice.enorm (0 : Lattice d) = 0 := by
1813        simp [Lattice.enorm, Lattice.toEuc]; rfl
1814      simp [h1, h0]
1815    · simp [hk, integral_cosMode_of_ne k hk]
1816  simp_rw [this]
1817  rw [tsum_ite_eq]
1818
1819
1820
1821theorem integral_Box_prod (f : Fin d → ℝ → ℝ) :
1822    ∫ y in Box d, ∏ i, f i (y i) = ∏ i, ∫ x in Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1), f i x := by
1823  have h : Box d = Set.univ.pi fun _ => Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1) := by
1824    ext y; simp [Box]
1825  rw [h, volume_pi, Measure.restrict_pi_pi]
1826  exact integral_fintype_prod_eq_prod (fun i x => f i x)
1827
1828theorem integral_gauss' (b : ℝ) :
1829    ∫ x : ℝ, Real.exp (-(b * x ^ 2)) = Real.sqrt (Real.pi / b) := by
1830  have := integral_gaussian b
1831  simpa only [neg_mul] using this
1832
1833theorem gauss_int_le {b : ℝ} (hb : 0 < b) :
1834    ∫ x in Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1), Real.exp (-(b * x ^ 2)) ≤ Real.sqrt (Real.pi / b) := by
1835  have hi : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => Real.exp (-(b * x ^ 2))) := by
1836    simpa using integrable_exp_neg_mul_sq hb
1837  have := setIntegral_le_integral (μ := (volume : Measure ℝ)) (s := Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1)) hi
1838    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => (Real.exp_pos _).le)
1839  rw [integral_gauss'] at this
1840  exact this
1841
1842theorem abs_mul_exp_le' {b : ℝ} (hb : 0 < b) (x : ℝ) :
1843    |x| * Real.exp (-(b / 2 * x ^ 2)) ≤ 1 / Real.sqrt b := by
1844  set y := Real.sqrt b * |x| with hy
1845  have hy0 : 0 ≤ y := by positivity
1846  have h1 : y ≤ Real.exp (y ^ 2 / 2) := by
1847    have := Real.add_one_le_exp (y ^ 2 / 2)
1848    nlinarith [sq_nonneg (y - 1)]
1849  have h2 : y ^ 2 = b * x ^ 2 := by
1850    rw [hy, mul_pow, Real.sq_sqrt hb.le, sq_abs]
1851  have h3 : y * Real.exp (-(y ^ 2 / 2)) ≤ 1 := by
1852    rw [Real.exp_neg, ← div_eq_mul_inv, div_le_one (Real.exp_pos _)]
1853    exact h1
1854  have hs : 0 < Real.sqrt b := Real.sqrt_pos.mpr hb
1855  rw [h2] at h3
1856  have e : -(b * x ^ 2 / 2) = -(b / 2 * x ^ 2) := by ring
1857  rw [e] at h3
1858  rw [le_div_iff₀ hs]
1859  calc |x| * Real.exp (-(b / 2 * x ^ 2)) * Real.sqrt b = (Real.sqrt b * |x|) * Real.exp (-(b / 2 * x ^ 2)) := by ring
1860    _ = y * Real.exp (-(b / 2 * x ^ 2)) := rfl
1861    _ ≤ 1 := h3
1862
1863theorem gauss_mom_le {b : ℝ} (hb : 0 < b) :
1864    ∫ x in Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1), |x| * Real.exp (-(b * x ^ 2)) ≤
1865      Real.sqrt (2 * Real.pi) / b := by
1866  have hs : 0 < Real.sqrt b := Real.sqrt_pos.mpr hb
1867  have hi : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => Real.exp (-(b / 2 * x ^ 2))) := by
1868    simpa using integrable_exp_neg_mul_sq (show 0 < b / 2 by positivity)
1869  have hcont : Continuous fun x : ℝ => |x| * Real.exp (-(b * x ^ 2)) := by fun_prop
1870  calc ∫ x in Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1), |x| * Real.exp (-(b * x ^ 2))
1871      ≤ ∫ x in Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1), (1 / Real.sqrt b) * Real.exp (-(b / 2 * x ^ 2)) := by
1872        refine setIntegral_mono_on (hcont.integrableOn_Ioc) (hi.const_mul _).integrableOn
1873          measurableSet_Ioc fun x _ => ?_
1874        have h1 := abs_mul_exp_le' hb x
1875        have h2 : Real.exp (-(b * x ^ 2)) = Real.exp (-(b / 2 * x ^ 2)) * Real.exp (-(b / 2 * x ^ 2)) := by
1876          rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
1877        rw [h2, ← mul_assoc]
1878        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h1 (Real.exp_pos _).le
1879    _ ≤ ∫ x : ℝ, (1 / Real.sqrt b) * Real.exp (-(b / 2 * x ^ 2)) :=
1880        setIntegral_le_integral (hi.const_mul _)
1881          (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by positivity)
1882    _ = Real.sqrt (2 * Real.pi) / b := by
1883        rw [integral_const_mul, integral_gauss']
1884        have : Real.sqrt (Real.pi / (b / 2)) = Real.sqrt (2 * Real.pi) / Real.sqrt b := by
1885          rw [← Real.sqrt_div (by positivity)]; congr 1; field_simp
1886        rw [this]
1887        have hbb : Real.sqrt b * Real.sqrt b = b := Real.mul_self_sqrt hb.le
1888        field_simp
1889        nlinarith
1890
1891
1892
1893/-- constant `B = ∑_n e^{-π² n²/8}` -/
1894def thetaB : ℝ := ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / 8) * (n : ℝ) ^ 2)
1895
1896theorem thetaB_nonneg : 0 ≤ thetaB := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
1897
1898/-- Gaussian majorant of the one-dimensional theta factor -/
1899def Gt (t y : ℝ) : ℝ :=
1900  Real.sqrt (Real.pi / t) * thetaB * Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * t) * y ^ 2))
1901
1902theorem Gt_nonneg (t y : ℝ) : 0 ≤ Gt t y := by
1903  unfold Gt
1904  have := thetaB_nonneg
1905  positivity
1906
1907theorem heatK_boxPt_le {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) (ht1 : t ≤ 1) {y : Fin d → ℝ} (hy : ∀ i, |y i| ≤ 1 / 2) :
1908    0 ≤ heatK d t (boxPt y) ∧ heatK d t (boxPt y) ≤ ∏ i, Gt t (y i) := by
1909  have h := theta_prod y ht
1910  unfold heatK
1911  rw [h]
1912  have hb := fun i => theta_factor_bound (y i) (hy i) ht ht1
1913  refine ⟨Finset.prod_nonneg fun i _ => (hb i).1, Finset.prod_le_prod₀ (fun i _ => (hb i).1)
1914    (fun i _ => ?_)⟩
1915  refine (hb i).2.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
1916  unfold Gt thetaB
1917  congr 2
1918  congr 1
1919  ring
1920
1921theorem Gt_int_le {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) :
1922    ∫ y in Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1), Gt t y ≤ Real.sqrt 2 * thetaB := by
1923  have hb : 0 < Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * t) := by positivity
1924  have h := gauss_int_le hb
1925  have hK : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt (Real.pi / t) * thetaB := by have := thetaB_nonneg; positivity
1926  have e : ∀ y : ℝ, Gt t y = (Real.sqrt (Real.pi / t) * thetaB) *
1927      Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * t) * y ^ 2)) := fun y => rfl
1928  simp_rw [e]
1929  rw [integral_const_mul]
1930  refine (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h hK).trans (le_of_eq ?_)
1931  have h1 : Real.pi / (Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * t)) = 2 * t / Real.pi := by
1932    field_simp
1933  rw [h1, mul_comm (Real.sqrt (Real.pi / t)), mul_assoc, ← Real.sqrt_mul (by positivity)]
1934  have h2 : Real.pi / t * (2 * t / Real.pi) = 2 := by field_simp
1935  rw [h2]; ring
1936
1937theorem Gt_mom_le {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) :
1938    ∫ y in Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1), |y| * Gt t y ≤
1939      (2 * Real.sqrt 2 / Real.pi * thetaB) * Real.sqrt t := by
1940  have hb : 0 < Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * t) := by positivity
1941  have h := gauss_mom_le hb
1942  have hK : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt (Real.pi / t) * thetaB := by have := thetaB_nonneg; positivity
1943  have e : ∀ y : ℝ, |y| * Gt t y = (Real.sqrt (Real.pi / t) * thetaB) *
1944      (|y| * Real.exp (-(Real.pi ^ 2 / (2 * t) * y ^ 2))) := fun y => by unfold Gt; ring
1945  simp_rw [e]
1946  rw [integral_const_mul]
1947  refine (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h hK).trans (le_of_eq ?_)
1948  have hs : 0 < Real.sqrt t := Real.sqrt_pos.mpr ht
1949  have hπ : 0 < Real.pi := Real.pi_pos
1950  have e1 : Real.sqrt (Real.pi / t) = Real.sqrt Real.pi / Real.sqrt t := Real.sqrt_div hπ.le t
1951  have e2 : Real.sqrt (2 * Real.pi) = Real.sqrt 2 * Real.sqrt Real.pi := Real.sqrt_mul (by norm_num) _
1952  have e3 : Real.sqrt Real.pi * Real.sqrt Real.pi = Real.pi := Real.mul_self_sqrt hπ.le
1953  have e4 : Real.sqrt t * Real.sqrt t = t := Real.mul_self_sqrt ht.le
1954  rw [e1, e2]
1955  field_simp
1956  rw [Real.sq_sqrt hπ.le, Real.sq_sqrt ht.le]
1957  ring
1958
1959
1960
1961theorem norm_toLp_le_sum_abs (y : Fin d → ℝ) : ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ ≤ ∑ i, |y i| := by
1962  rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]
1963  refine Real.sqrt_le_iff.mpr ⟨Finset.sum_nonneg (fun i _ => abs_nonneg _), ?_⟩
1964  have := Finset.sum_sq_le_sq_sum_of_nonneg (s := Finset.univ) (f := fun i => |y i|)
1965    (fun i _ => abs_nonneg _)
1966  simpa [sq_abs] using this
1967
1968theorem Box_subset_Icc (d : ℕ) :
1969    Box d ⊆ Set.Icc (fun _ : Fin d => (-1 / 2 : ℝ)) (fun _ => 1 / 2) := by
1970  intro y hy
1971  refine ⟨fun i => ?_, fun i => ?_⟩ <;> have := Box_abs_le hy i <;> rw [abs_le] at this <;> linarith [this.1, this.2]
1972
1973theorem centeredRep_boxPt {y : Fin d → ℝ} (hy : ∀ i, |y i| ≤ 1 / 2) :
1974    ‖KernelGrad.centeredRep (boxPt y)‖ = ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ := by
1975  have h := KernelGrad.tdist_mk (d := d) (y := (WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)) hy
1976  rw [KernelGrad.tdist_zero_right] at h
1977  exact h

1979/-- the factors of the first-moment majorant -/
1980def gMom (t : ℝ) (j i : Fin d) (y : ℝ) : ℝ := Gt t y * (if i = j then |y| else 1)
1981
1982theorem gMom_nonneg (t : ℝ) (j i : Fin d) (y : ℝ) : 0 ≤ gMom t j i y := by
1983  unfold gMom
1984  have := Gt_nonneg t y
1985  split_ifs <;> positivity
1986
1987theorem continuous_Gt (t : ℝ) : Continuous (Gt t) := by
1988  unfold Gt; fun_prop
1989
1990theorem continuous_gMom (t : ℝ) (j i : Fin d) : Continuous (gMom t j i) := by
1991  unfold gMom
1992  by_cases h : i = j
1993  · simp only [h, ite_true]; have := continuous_Gt t; fun_prop
1994  · simp only [h, ite_false]; have := continuous_Gt t; fun_prop
1995
1996theorem gMom_int_le {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) (j i : Fin d) :
1997    ∫ y in Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1), gMom t j i y ≤
1998      if i = j then (2 * Real.sqrt 2 / Real.pi * thetaB) * Real.sqrt t else Real.sqrt 2 * thetaB := by
1999  by_cases h : i = j
2000  · simp only [h, ite_true]
2001    refine le_trans (le_of_eq ?_) (Gt_mom_le ht)
2002    refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc fun y _ => ?_
2003    simp only [gMom, ite_true]; ring
2004  · simp only [h, ite_false]
2005    refine le_trans (le_of_eq ?_) (Gt_int_le ht)
2006    refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc fun y _ => ?_
2007    simp only [gMom, h, ite_false, mul_one]
2008
2009theorem heat_moment (hd : 1 ≤ d) :
2010    ∃ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M ∧ ∀ t : ℝ, 0 < t → t ≤ 1 →
2011      ∫ y : Torus d, heatK d t y * ‖KernelGrad.centeredRep y‖ ≤ M * Real.sqrt t := by
2012  have hB := thetaB_nonneg
2013  refine ⟨d * (Real.sqrt 2 * thetaB) ^ (d - 1) * (2 * Real.sqrt 2 / Real.pi * thetaB), by positivity,
2014    fun t ht ht1 => ?_⟩
2015  rw [integral_torus_box]
2016  set M₁ := Real.sqrt 2 * thetaB with hM₁
2017  set M₂ := (2 * Real.sqrt 2 / Real.pi * thetaB) * Real.sqrt t with hM₂
2018  have hpt : ∀ y ∈ Box d, heatK d t (boxPt y) * ‖KernelGrad.centeredRep (boxPt y)‖ ≤
2019      ∑ j, ∏ i, gMom t j i (y i) := by
2020    intro y hy
2021    have hy' := Box_abs_le hy
2022    obtain ⟨h0, h1⟩ := heatK_boxPt_le ht ht1 hy'
2023    rw [centeredRep_boxPt hy']
2024    have hn : ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ ≤ ∑ i, |y i| := norm_toLp_le_sum_abs y
2025    have hprod0 : 0 ≤ ∏ i, Gt t (y i) := Finset.prod_nonneg fun i _ => Gt_nonneg t _
2026    have e : ∑ j, ∏ i, gMom t j i (y i) = (∏ i, Gt t (y i)) * ∑ j, |y j| := by
2027      rw [Finset.mul_sum]
2028      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
2029      simp only [gMom]
2030      rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
2031      congr 1
2032      rw [← Finset.mul_prod_erase Finset.univ (fun i => if i = j then |y i| else 1) (Finset.mem_univ j)]
2033      simp only [ite_true]
2034      rw [Finset.prod_congr rfl (fun i hi => if_neg (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hi))]
2035      simp
2036    rw [e]
2037    calc heatK d t (boxPt y) * ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖
2038        ≤ (∏ i, Gt t (y i)) * ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h1 (norm_nonneg _)
2039      _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hn hprod0
2040  have hcontj : ∀ j, Continuous fun y : Fin d → ℝ => ∏ i, gMom t j i (y i) := fun j =>
2041    continuous_finset_prod _ fun i _ => (continuous_gMom t j i).comp (continuous_apply i)
2042  have hIcc : ∀ {f : (Fin d → ℝ) → ℝ}, Continuous f → IntegrableOn f (Box d) := fun hf =>
2043    (hf.integrableOn_Icc (a := fun _ : Fin d => (-1 / 2 : ℝ)) (b := fun _ => 1 / 2)).mono_set
2044      (Box_subset_Icc d)
2045  have hfint : IntegrableOn (fun y : Fin d → ℝ =>
2046      heatK d t (boxPt y) * ‖KernelGrad.centeredRep (boxPt y)‖) (Box d) := by
2047    have hc : Continuous fun y : Fin d → ℝ => heatK d t (boxPt y) * ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ :=
2048      ((continuous_heatK ht).comp (continuous_boxPt d)).mul
2049        (continuous_norm.comp (by fun_prop))
2050    refine (hIcc hc).congr_fun (fun y hy => ?_) (measurableSet_Box d)
2051    simp only
2052    rw [centeredRep_boxPt (Box_abs_le hy)]
2053  calc ∫ y in Box d, heatK d t (boxPt y) * ‖KernelGrad.centeredRep (boxPt y)‖
2054      ≤ ∫ y in Box d, ∑ j, ∏ i, gMom t j i (y i) :=
2055        setIntegral_mono_on hfint (integrable_finset_sum _ fun j _ => hIcc (hcontj j))
2056          (measurableSet_Box d) hpt
2057    _ = ∑ j, ∫ y in Box d, ∏ i, gMom t j i (y i) :=
2058        integral_finset_sum _ fun j _ => hIcc (hcontj j)
2059    _ = ∑ j : Fin d, ∏ i, ∫ x in Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1), gMom t j i x := by
2060        refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
2061        exact integral_Box_prod (fun i x => gMom t j i x)
2062    _ ≤ ∑ j : Fin d, M₂ * M₁ ^ (d - 1) := by
2063        refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun j _ => ?_
2064        calc ∏ i, ∫ x in Ioc (-1 / 2 : ℝ) (-1 / 2 + 1), gMom t j i x
2065            ≤ ∏ i : Fin d, (if i = j then M₂ else M₁) :=
2066              Finset.prod_le_prod₀ (fun i _ => setIntegral_nonneg measurableSet_Ioc
2067                fun x _ => gMom_nonneg t j i x) (fun i _ => gMom_int_le ht j i)
2068          _ = M₂ * M₁ ^ (d - 1) := by
2069              rw [← Finset.mul_prod_erase Finset.univ (fun i => if i = j then M₂ else M₁)
2070                (Finset.mem_univ j)]
2071              simp only [ite_true]
2072              rw [Finset.prod_congr rfl (fun i hi => if_neg (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hi)),
2073                Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_erase_of_mem (Finset.mem_univ j), Finset.card_univ,
2074                Fintype.card_fin]
2075    _ = _ := by
2076        rw [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul, hM₂]; ring
2077

2080theorem tdist_eq_sqrt (x y : Torus d) :
2081    KernelGrad.tdist x y = Real.sqrt (∑ i, ‖(x - y) i‖ ^ 2) := by
2082  unfold KernelGrad.tdist
2083  rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]
2084  congr 1
2085  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
2086  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, sq_abs, KernelGrad.norm_apply_eq, sq_abs]
2087
2088theorem continuous_tdist_left (y : Torus d) : Continuous fun x : Torus d => KernelGrad.tdist x y := by
2089  simp_rw [tdist_eq_sqrt]
2090  fun_prop
2091
2092theorem continuous_tdist_zero : Continuous fun x : Torus d => KernelGrad.tdist x 0 :=
2093  continuous_tdist_left 0
2094
2095theorem tdist_self (x : Torus d) : KernelGrad.tdist x x = 0 := by
2096  rw [tdist_eq_sqrt]; simp

2098theorem continuous_of_isLipschitz_aux {L : ℝ} {ψ : Torus d → ℝ} (h : KernelGrad.IsLipschitz L ψ) :
2099    Continuous ψ := by
2100  refine continuous_iff_continuousAt.2 fun x => ?_
2101  rw [ContinuousAt, tendsto_iff_dist_tendsto_zero]
2102  refine squeeze_zero (fun y => dist_nonneg) (fun y => ?_)
2103    (g := fun y => |L| * KernelGrad.tdist y x) ?_
2104  · rw [Real.dist_eq]
2105    calc |ψ y - ψ x| ≤ L * KernelGrad.tdist y x := h y x
2106      _ ≤ |L| * KernelGrad.tdist y x :=
2107          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (le_abs_self L) (KernelGrad.tdist_nonneg _ _)
2108  · have := ((continuous_tdist_left x).const_mul |L|).tendsto x
2109    simpa [tdist_self] using this

2111/-- the heat mollification `P_t ψ (x) = ∫ p_t(y) ψ(x - y) dy` -/
2112def heatP (d : ℕ) (t : ℝ) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (x : Torus d) : ℝ :=
2113  ∫ y, heatK d t y * ψ (x - y)
2114
2115theorem heatP_approx (hd : 1 ≤ d) :
2116    ∃ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M ∧ ∀ t : ℝ, 0 < t → t ≤ 1 → ∀ (L : ℝ) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ), 0 ≤ L →
2117      KernelGrad.IsLipschitz L ψ → ∀ x : Torus d, |ψ x - heatP d t ψ x| ≤ L * (M * Real.sqrt t) := by
2118  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := heat_moment hd
2119  refine ⟨M, hM0, fun t ht ht1 L ψ hL hψ x => ?_⟩
2120  have hc := continuous_of_isLipschitz_aux hψ
2121  have hK := continuous_heatK (d := d) ht
2122  have hint1 : Integrable (fun y => heatK d t y * ψ (x - y)) :=
2123    (hK.mul (hc.comp (continuous_const.sub continuous_id))).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
2124      (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2125  have hint2 : Integrable (fun y : Torus d => heatK d t y * KernelGrad.tdist y 0) :=
2126    (hK.mul continuous_tdist_zero).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2127  have hint0 : Integrable (fun y : Torus d => heatK d t y * ψ x) :=
2128    (hK.mul continuous_const).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2129  have h2 : ψ x - heatP d t ψ x = ∫ y, heatK d t y * (ψ x - ψ (x - y)) := by
2130    have e : ∫ y, heatK d t y * (ψ x - ψ (x - y)) =
2131        (∫ y, heatK d t y * ψ x) - ∫ y, heatK d t y * ψ (x - y) := by
2132      simp_rw [mul_sub]; exact integral_sub hint0 hint1
2133    rw [e, integral_mul_const, integral_heatK ht, one_mul]
2134    rfl
2135  rw [h2, ← Real.norm_eq_abs]
2136  calc ‖∫ y, heatK d t y * (ψ x - ψ (x - y))‖
2137      ≤ ∫ y, L * (heatK d t y * KernelGrad.tdist y 0) :=
2138        norm_integral_le_of_norm_le (hint2.const_mul L) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
2139    _ = L * ∫ y, heatK d t y * KernelGrad.tdist y 0 := integral_const_mul _ _
2140    _ ≤ L * (M * Real.sqrt t) := by
2141        refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ hL
2142        have := hM t ht ht1
2143        simpa [KernelGrad.tdist_zero_right] using this
2144  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (heatK_nonneg ht y)]
2145  have h3 := hψ x (x - y)
2146  have h4 : KernelGrad.tdist x (x - y) = KernelGrad.tdist y 0 := by
2147    unfold KernelGrad.tdist; simp
2148  rw [h4] at h3
2149  calc heatK d t y * |ψ x - ψ (x - y)| ≤ heatK d t y * (L * KernelGrad.tdist y 0) :=
2150        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h3 (heatK_nonneg ht y)
2151    _ = _ := by ring

2155theorem fcoef_norm_le {ψ : Torus d → ℝ} {M : ℝ} (hM : ∀ x, |ψ x| ≤ M) (k : Lattice d) :
2156    ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k‖ ≤ M := by
2157  unfold Torus.fcoef
2158  have h := norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (μ := (volume : Measure (Torus d)))
2159    (f := fun x => Torus.mode (-k) x * (ψ x : ℂ)) (C := M) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by
2160      rw [norm_mul, norm_mode, one_mul, Complex.norm_real, Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact hM x)
2161  simpa using h

2163theorem integral_cos_mul_shift {ψ : Torus d → ℝ} (hc : Continuous ψ) (k : Lattice d) (x : Torus d) :
2164    ∫ y, Torus.cosMode k y * ψ (x - y) = (Torus.mode k x * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re := by
2165  have h1 : ∀ y, Torus.cosMode k y * ψ (x - y) = ((Torus.mode k y) * ((ψ (x - y) : ℝ) : ℂ)).re := by
2166    intro y
2167    unfold Torus.cosMode
2168    rw [Complex.re_mul_ofReal]
2169  simp_rw [h1]
2170  have hc' : Continuous fun y => Torus.mode k y * ((ψ (x - y) : ℝ) : ℂ) :=
2171    (Torus.continuous_mode k).mul
2172      (Complex.continuous_ofReal.comp (hc.comp (continuous_const.sub continuous_id)))
2173  have hint : Integrable (fun y => Torus.mode k y * ((ψ (x - y) : ℝ) : ℂ)) :=
2174    hc'.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2175  have := integral_re hint
2176  simp only [RCLike.re_to_complex] at this
2177  rw [this]
2178  congr 1
2179  have h2 : ∫ y, Torus.mode k y * ((ψ (x - y) : ℝ) : ℂ) =
2180      ∫ z, Torus.mode k (x - z) * ((ψ z : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
2181    haveI : (volume : Measure (Torus d)).IsNegInvariant :=
2182      MeasureTheory.Measure.pi.isNegInvariant (fun _ : Fin d => (volume : Measure UnitAddCircle))
2183    rw [← integral_sub_left_eq_self (fun z => Torus.mode k (x - z) * ((ψ z : ℝ) : ℂ)) volume x]
2184    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
2185    simp
2186  rw [h2]
2187  have h3 : ∀ z, Torus.mode k (x - z) * ((ψ z : ℝ) : ℂ) =
2188      Torus.mode k x * (Torus.mode (-k) z * ((ψ z : ℝ) : ℂ)) := by
2189    intro z; rw [mode_sub_conj, ← Torus.mode_neg]; ring
2190  simp_rw [h3]
2191  rw [integral_const_mul]
2192  rfl

2196theorem heatP_series {ψ : Torus d → ℝ} (hc : Continuous ψ) {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) (x : Torus d) :
2197    heatP d t ψ x = ∑' k : Lattice d,
2198      Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (Torus.mode k x * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re := by
2199  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := exists_abs_bound hc
2200  unfold heatP heatK
2201  have e : ∀ y : Torus d, (∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k y) *
2202      ψ (x - y) = ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
2203        (Torus.cosMode k y * ψ (x - y)) := by
2204    intro y; rw [← tsum_mul_right]; congr 1; funext k; ring
2205  simp_rw [e]
2206  rw [integral_tsum_c (summable_heatK_coef ht) (volume : Measure (Torus d))
2207    (fun k y => Torus.cosMode k y * ψ (x - y))
2208    (fun k => (Torus.continuous_cosMode k).mul (hc.comp (continuous_const.sub continuous_id)))
2209    (B := M) (fun k y => by
2210      rw [abs_mul]
2211      calc |Torus.cosMode k y| * |ψ (x - y)| ≤ 1 * M :=
2212            mul_le_mul (abs_cosMode_le k y) (hM _) (abs_nonneg _) zero_le_one
2213        _ = M := one_mul _)]
2214  refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
2215  rw [integral_cos_mul_shift hc k x]
2216
2217theorem integral_heatP_eq {ψ : Torus d → ℝ} (hc : Continuous ψ) {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t)
2218    (Qm : Measure (Torus d)) [IsFiniteMeasure Qm] :
2219    ∫ x, heatP d t ψ x ∂Qm = ∑' k : Lattice d,
2220      Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * ((∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂Qm) * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re := by
2221  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := exists_abs_bound hc
2222  simp_rw [heatP_series hc ht]
2223  rw [integral_tsum_c (summable_heatK_coef ht) Qm (fun k x => (Torus.mode k x * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re)
2224    (fun k => Complex.continuous_re.comp ((Torus.continuous_mode k).mul continuous_const))
2225    (B := M) (fun k x => by
2226      refine (Complex.abs_re_le_norm _).trans ?_
2227      rw [norm_mul, norm_mode, one_mul]
2228      exact fcoef_norm_le hM k)]
2229  refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
2230  congr 1
2231  have hint : Integrable (fun x => Torus.mode k x * Torus.fcoef ψ k) Qm :=
2232    ((Torus.continuous_mode k).mul continuous_const).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport
2233      (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2234  have := integral_re hint
2235  simp only [RCLike.re_to_complex] at this
2236  rw [this, integral_mul_const]

2240theorem integrableOn_gam {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) {m : ℝ} (hm : 0 < m) :
2241    IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(m * u))) (Ioi 0) := by
2242  have := integrableOn_rpow_mul_exp_neg_mul_rpow (p := 1) (s := a - 1) (b := m)
2243    (by linarith) one_pos hm
2244  refine this.congr_fun (fun t _ => ?_) measurableSet_Ioi
2245  simp only [Real.rpow_one]
2246  ring_nf
2247
2248theorem one_add_rpow_ge {x b : ℝ} (hx : 0 ≤ x) (hb0 : -1 ≤ b) (hb1 : b ≤ 0) :
2249    Real.exp (-x) ≤ (1 + x) ^ b := by
2250  have h1 : (0:ℝ) < 1 + x := by linarith
2251  have h2 : Real.log (1 + x) ≤ x := by
2252    have := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos h1; linarith
2253  have h3 : 0 ≤ Real.log (1 + x) := Real.log_nonneg (by linarith)
2254  rw [Real.rpow_def_of_pos h1]
2255  apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
2256  nlinarith
2257
2258theorem intT_lower {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) : ∃ c : ℝ, 0 < c ∧ ∀ {ε m : ℝ}, 0 < ε → 1 ≤ m →
2259    c * (Real.exp (-(2 * ε * m)) * m ^ (-a)) ≤ ∫ u in Ioi ε, u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(m * u)) := by
2260  refine ⟨Real.exp (-2) * min 1 ((2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1)), by positivity, fun {ε m} hε hm => ?_⟩
2261  have hm0 : 0 < m := by linarith
2262  set J : Set ℝ := Ioc (ε + 1 / m) (ε + 2 / m) with hJ
2263  have hint : IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(m * u))) (Ioi ε) :=
2264    (integrableOn_gam ha hm0).mono_set (Ioi_subset_Ioi hε.le)
2265  have hJsub : J ⊆ Ioi ε := fun u hu => by
2266    have : 0 < 1 / m := by positivity
2267    exact lt_trans (by linarith) hu.1
2268  have hnn : ∀ u ∈ Ioi ε, 0 ≤ u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(m * u)) := fun u hu => by
2269    have hu0 : 0 < u := lt_trans hε hu
2270    have := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 (a - 1)
2271    positivity
2272  have h1 : ∫ u in J, u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(m * u)) ≤
2273      ∫ u in Ioi ε, u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(m * u)) :=
2274    setIntegral_mono_set hint ((ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr
2275      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hnn)) hJsub.eventuallyLE
2276  refine le_trans ?_ h1
2277  -- lower bound on `J`
2278  have hpos : 0 < 1 / m := by positivity
2279  have hlen : ε + 2 / m - (ε + 1 / m) = 1 / m := by ring
2280  have hvol : (volume J).toReal = 1 / m := by
2281    rw [hJ, Real.volume_Ioc, hlen, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hpos.le]
2282  have hfin : volume J ≠ ⊤ := by
2283    rw [hJ, Real.volume_Ioc]; exact ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
2284  set low : ℝ := if 1 ≤ a then (1 / m) ^ (a - 1) else (2 / m) ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(ε * m / 2)) with hlow
2285  have hpt : ∀ u ∈ J, low * Real.exp (-(m * ε + 2)) ≤ u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(m * u)) := by
2286    intro u hu
2287    have hu0 : 0 < u := lt_trans hε (hJsub hu)
2288    have hexp : Real.exp (-(m * ε + 2)) ≤ Real.exp (-(m * u)) := by
2289      apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
2290      have : m * u ≤ m * ε + 2 := by
2291        have h2 : u ≤ ε + 2 / m := hu.2
2292        calc m * u ≤ m * (ε + 2 / m) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 hm0.le
2293          _ = m * ε + 2 := by field_simp
2294      linarith
2295    have hlow0 : 0 ≤ low := by
2296      rw [hlow]; split_ifs <;> positivity
2297    have hlowle : low ≤ u ^ (a - 1) := by
2298      rw [hlow]
2299      split_ifs with ha1
2300      · have : 1 / m ≤ u := by
2301          have : 0 < ε := hε
2302          linarith [hu.1]
2303        exact Real.rpow_le_rpow (by positivity) this (by linarith)
2304      · push Not at ha1
2305        have hu2 : u ≤ ε + 2 / m := hu.2
2306        have h3 : (ε + 2 / m) ^ (a - 1) ≤ u ^ (a - 1) :=
2307          Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hu0 hu2 (by linarith)
2308        refine le_trans ?_ h3
2309        have h4 : ε + 2 / m = (2 / m) * (1 + ε * m / 2) := by field_simp; ring
2310        rw [h4, Real.mul_rpow (by positivity) (by positivity)]
2311        refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (by positivity)
2312        have := one_add_rpow_ge (x := ε * m / 2) (b := a - 1) (by positivity) (by linarith) (by linarith)
2313        exact this
2314    exact mul_le_mul hlowle hexp (Real.exp_pos _).le (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hu0 _).le
2315  have h2 : (low * Real.exp (-(m * ε + 2))) * (1 / m) ≤
2316      ∫ u in J, u ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(m * u)) := by
2317    have hc : IntegrableOn (fun _ : ℝ => low * Real.exp (-(m * ε + 2))) J := integrableOn_const hfin
2318    have := setIntegral_mono_on hc (hint.mono_set hJsub) measurableSet_Ioc hpt
2319    rw [setIntegral_const, Measure.real, hvol, smul_eq_mul] at this
2320    linarith
2321  refine le_trans ?_ h2
2322  have e1 : m ^ (-a) = m ^ (-(a - 1)) * (1 / m) := by
2323    rw [show -a = -(a - 1) + (-1) by ring, Real.rpow_add hm0, Real.rpow_neg_one]; field_simp
2324  have hexp1 : Real.exp (-(2 * ε * m)) ≤ Real.exp (-(m * ε)) := by
2325    apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr; nlinarith [mul_pos hε hm0]
2326  have hmin1 : min 1 ((2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1)) ≤ 1 := min_le_left _ _
2327  have hmin2 : min 1 ((2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1)) ≤ (2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1) := min_le_right _ _
2328  have hmP : 0 < m ^ (-a) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hm0 _
2329  have hmin0 : 0 < min 1 ((2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1)) := by positivity
2330  rw [hlow]
2331  split_ifs with ha1
2332  · have e2 : (1 / m) ^ (a - 1) = m ^ (-(a - 1)) := by
2333      rw [one_div, Real.inv_rpow hm0.le, Real.rpow_neg hm0.le]
2334    rw [e2]
2335    have e3 : Real.exp (-(m * ε + 2)) = Real.exp (-2) * Real.exp (-(m * ε)) := by
2336      rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
2337    rw [e3]
2338    have : m ^ (-(a - 1)) * (Real.exp (-2) * Real.exp (-(m * ε))) * (1 / m) =
2339        Real.exp (-2) * Real.exp (-(m * ε)) * m ^ (-a) := by rw [e1]; ring
2340    rw [this]
2341    have h5 : 0 < Real.exp (-2) := Real.exp_pos _
2342    have h6 : 0 ≤ Real.exp (-(2 * ε * m)) * m ^ (-a) := by positivity
2343    calc Real.exp (-2) * min 1 ((2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1)) * (Real.exp (-(2 * ε * m)) * m ^ (-a))
2344        ≤ Real.exp (-2) * 1 * (Real.exp (-(m * ε)) * m ^ (-a)) := by
2345          refine mul_le_mul (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hmin1 h5.le)
2346            (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hexp1 hmP.le) h6 (by positivity)
2347      _ = _ := by ring
2348  · push Not at ha1
2349    have e2 : (2 / m) ^ (a - 1) = (2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1) * m ^ (-(a - 1)) := by
2350      rw [Real.div_rpow (by norm_num) hm0.le, Real.rpow_neg hm0.le, div_eq_mul_inv]
2351    rw [e2]
2352    have e3 : Real.exp (-(m * ε + 2)) = Real.exp (-2) * Real.exp (-(m * ε)) := by
2353      rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
2354    rw [e3]
2355    have hexp2 : Real.exp (-(2 * ε * m)) ≤ Real.exp (-(ε * m / 2)) * Real.exp (-(m * ε)) := by
2356      rw [← Real.exp_add]; apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr; nlinarith [mul_pos hε hm0]
2357    have : (2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1) * m ^ (-(a - 1)) * Real.exp (-(ε * m / 2)) *
2358        (Real.exp (-2) * Real.exp (-(m * ε))) * (1 / m) =
2359        (2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-2) * (Real.exp (-(ε * m / 2)) * Real.exp (-(m * ε)) * m ^ (-a)) := by
2360      rw [e1]; ring
2361    rw [this]
2362    have h5 : 0 < Real.exp (-2) := Real.exp_pos _
2363    have h6 : 0 ≤ Real.exp (-(2 * ε * m)) * m ^ (-a) := by positivity
2364    have h7 : 0 < (2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1) := by positivity
2365    calc Real.exp (-2) * min 1 ((2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1)) * (Real.exp (-(2 * ε * m)) * m ^ (-a))
2366        ≤ Real.exp (-2) * (2 : ℝ) ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-(ε * m / 2)) * Real.exp (-(m * ε)) * m ^ (-a)) := by
2367          refine mul_le_mul (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hmin2 h5.le)
2368            (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hexp2 hmP.le) h6 (by positivity)
2369      _ = _ := by ring

2373theorem subA_pos_one (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) : 0 < subA d s := by
2374  have ha : 0 < ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 := by
2375    have : (s : ℝ) < d := hsd
2376    linarith
2377  unfold subA
2378  have := rieszConst_pos_one hd hs0 hsd
2379  have := Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos ha
2380  positivity

2382theorem cT_lower (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
2383    ∃ c : ℝ, 0 < c ∧ ∀ {ε : ℝ}, 0 < ε → ∀ k : Lattice d, k ≠ 0 →
2384      c * (Real.exp (-(2 * ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) * (Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) ^ (-(((d : ℝ) - s) / 2))) ≤
2385        cT d s ε k := by
2386  have ha : 0 < ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 := by
2387    have : (s : ℝ) < d := hsd
2388    linarith
2389  have hA := subA_pos_one hd hs0 hsd
2390  obtain ⟨c₁, hc₁, h₁⟩ := intT_lower ha
2391  refine ⟨subA d s * c₁, by positivity, fun {ε} hε k hk => ?_⟩
2392  have hm : 1 ≤ Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 := by
2393    have := one_le_enorm k hk; nlinarith
2394  have := h₁ hε hm
2395  unfold cT
2396  rw [if_neg hk]
2397  calc subA d s * c₁ * (Real.exp (-(2 * ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) * (Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) ^ (-(((d : ℝ) - s) / 2)))
2398      = subA d s * (c₁ * (Real.exp (-(2 * ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) *
2399          (Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) ^ (-(((d : ℝ) - s) / 2)))) := by ring
2400    _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this hA.le

2404theorem heatP_continuous {ψ : Torus d → ℝ} (hc : Continuous ψ) {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) :
2405    Continuous (heatP d t ψ) := by
2406  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := exists_abs_bound hc
2407  have : heatP d t ψ = fun x => ∑' k : Lattice d,
2408      Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (Torus.mode k x * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re :=
2409    funext (heatP_series hc ht)
2410  rw [this]
2411  refine continuous_tsum (u := fun k : Lattice d => Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * M)
2412    (fun k => continuous_const.mul (Complex.continuous_re.comp
2413      ((Torus.continuous_mode k).mul continuous_const)))
2414    ((summable_gauss_lattice d t ht).mul_right M) (fun k x => ?_)
2415  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
2416  refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (Real.exp_pos _).le
2417  refine (Complex.abs_re_le_norm _).trans ?_
2418  rw [norm_mul, norm_mode, one_mul]
2419  exact fcoef_norm_le hM k

2421/-- the terms of the homogeneous Sobolev series, extended by `0` at the origin -/
2422def homTerm (d : ℕ) (a : ℝ) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (k : Lattice d) : ℝ :=
2423  if k = 0 then 0 else (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (2 * a) * ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k‖ ^ 2
2424
2425theorem homSobolevSq_eq_tsum (a : ℝ) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) :
2426    homSobolevSq a ψ = ∑' k : Lattice d, homTerm d a ψ k := by
2427  unfold homSobolevSq
2428  have h := tsum_subtype ({k : Lattice d | k ≠ 0}) (fun k : Lattice d =>
2429    (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (2 * a) * ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k‖ ^ 2)
2430  refine h.trans (tsum_congr fun k => ?_)
2431  unfold homTerm
2432  simp only [Set.indicator_apply, Set.mem_setOf_eq]
2433  by_cases hk : k = 0 <;> simp [hk]
2434
2435theorem summable_homTerm {a : ℝ} {ψ : Torus d → ℝ} (h : HasFiniteHomSobolev a ψ) :
2436    Summable (homTerm d a ψ) := by
2437  have h' := (summable_subtype_iff_indicator (s := {k : Lattice d | k ≠ 0})
2438    (f := fun k : Lattice d => (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (2 * a) * ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k‖ ^ 2)).mp h
2439  refine h'.congr fun k => ?_
2440  unfold homTerm
2441  simp only [Set.indicator_apply, Set.mem_setOf_eq]
2442  by_cases hk : k = 0 <;> simp [hk]
2443

2445
2446theorem tsum_mul_le_sqrt {ι : Type*} {f g : ι → ℝ} (hf0 : ∀ i, 0 ≤ f i) (hg0 : ∀ i, 0 ≤ g i)
2447    (hf : Summable fun i => f i ^ 2) (hg : Summable fun i => g i ^ 2) :
2448    Summable (fun i => f i * g i) ∧
2449      ∑' i, f i * g i ≤ Real.sqrt (∑' i, f i ^ 2) * Real.sqrt (∑' i, g i ^ 2) := by
2450  have hf' : Summable fun i => f i ^ (2 : ℝ) := by simpa [Real.rpow_two] using hf
2451  have hg' : Summable fun i => g i ^ (2 : ℝ) := by simpa [Real.rpow_two] using hg
2452  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := Real.summable_and_inner_le_Lp_mul_Lq_tsum_of_nonneg
2453    Real.HolderConjugate.two_two hf0 hg0 hf' hg'
2454  refine ⟨h1, ?_⟩
2455  simp only [Real.rpow_two] at h2
2456  simpa [Real.sqrt_eq_rpow] using h2
2457

2460theorem weight_sq (a : ℝ) {x y : ℝ} (hx : 0 ≤ x) (hy : 0 ≤ y) :
2461    ((x * y) ^ (-a)) ^ 2 = x ^ (-(2 * a)) * (y ^ 2) ^ (-a) := by
2462  have h1 : ((x * y) ^ (-a)) ^ 2 = (x * y) ^ (-a * 2) := by
2463    rw [Real.rpow_mul (by positivity), Real.rpow_two]
2464  have h2 : (y ^ 2) ^ (-a) = y ^ (2 * (-a)) := by
2465    rw [show (y ^ 2 : ℝ) = y ^ ((2 : ℕ) : ℝ) by rw [Real.rpow_natCast], ← Real.rpow_mul hy]
2466    norm_num
2467  rw [h1, h2, Real.mul_rpow hx hy]
2468  congr 2 <;> ring
2469
2470theorem rpow_sq_eq (a : ℝ) {z : ℝ} (hz : 0 ≤ z) : (z ^ a) ^ 2 = z ^ (2 * a) := by
2471  rw [← Real.rpow_two, ← Real.rpow_mul hz]
2472  congr 1; ring
2473
2474theorem delta_zero {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d))
2475    [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] :
2476    ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) • ∑ i, Torus.mode (0 : Lattice d) (X i) - ∫ x, Torus.mode (0 : Lattice d) x ∂μ = 0 := by
2477  have hN0 : (N : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hN.ne'
2478  simp [Torus.mode_zero]
2479  have hNC : (N : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hN0
2480  rw [inv_mul_cancel₀ hNC, sub_self]
2481
2482theorem delta_norm_le {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d))
2483    [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (k : Lattice d) :
2484    ‖((N : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) • ∑ i, Torus.mode k (X i) - ∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂μ‖ ≤ 2 := by
2485  have := empirical_isProbabilityMeasure hN X
2486  rw [← integral_empirical_mode hN X k]
2487  refine (norm_sub_le _ _).trans ?_
2488  have h1 : ∀ (Qm : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure Qm], ‖∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂Qm‖ ≤ 1 := by
2489    intro Qm _
2490    refine (norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (C := 1) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)).trans ?_
2491    · rw [norm_mode]
2492    · simp
2493  linarith [h1 (empirical X), h1 μ]

2497/-- the Fourier coefficients of the signed measure `μ_N - μ` (conjugated convention: `∫ e_k d(μ_N-μ)`). -/
2498def deltaF {N : ℕ} (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (k : Lattice d) : ℂ :=
2499  ((N : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) • ∑ i, Torus.mode k (X i) - ∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂μ
2500
2501theorem Esq_eq_tsum {N : ℕ} (ε : ℝ) (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) :
2502    Esq d s ε X μ = ∑' k : Lattice d, cT d s ε k * ‖deltaF X μ k‖ ^ 2 := rfl
2503
2504theorem fourier_side_bound (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
2505    ∃ Cl : ℝ, 0 ≤ Cl ∧ ∀ {ε : ℝ}, 0 < ε → ∀ {N : ℕ}, 0 < N → ∀ (X : Config d N)
2506      (μ : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {ψ : Torus d → ℝ}, Continuous ψ →
2507      HasFiniteHomSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ →
2508      |∫ x, heatP d ε ψ x ∂(empirical X) - ∫ x, heatP d ε ψ x ∂μ| ≤
2509        Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * Real.sqrt (Cl * Esq d s ε X μ) := by
2510  have ha : 0 < ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 := by
2511    have : (s : ℝ) < d := hsd
2512    linarith
2513  obtain ⟨c, hc, hcl⟩ := cT_lower hd hs0 hsd
2514  refine ⟨(2 * Real.pi) ^ (-(2 * (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2))) / c, by positivity,
2515    fun {ε} hε {N} hN X μ _ {ψ} hψ hhom => ?_⟩
2516  haveI := empirical_isProbabilityMeasure hN X
2517  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := exists_abs_bound hψ
2518  -- Step (i): the Fourier expression of the difference
2519  have hsum : ∀ (z : Lattice d → ℂ), (∀ k, ‖z k‖ ≤ 1) →
2520      Summable fun k : Lattice d => Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (z k * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re := by
2521    intro z hz
2522    refine Summable.of_norm_bounded ((summable_gauss_lattice d ε hε).mul_right M) fun k => ?_
2523    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
2524    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (Real.exp_pos _).le
2525    refine (Complex.abs_re_le_norm _).trans ?_
2526    rw [norm_mul]
2527    calc ‖z k‖ * ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k‖ ≤ 1 * M :=
2528          mul_le_mul (hz k) (fcoef_norm_le hM k) (norm_nonneg _) zero_le_one
2529      _ = M := one_mul _
2530  have hnorm1 : ∀ (Qm : Measure (Torus d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure Qm] (k : Lattice d),
2531      ‖∫ x, Torus.mode k x ∂Qm‖ ≤ 1 := by
2532    intro Qm _ k
2533    refine (norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (C := 1) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)).trans ?_
2534    · rw [norm_mode]
2535    · simp
2536  have hstep1 : ∫ x, heatP d ε ψ x ∂(empirical X) - ∫ x, heatP d ε ψ x ∂μ =
2537      ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (deltaF X μ k * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re := by
2538    rw [integral_heatP_eq hψ hε (empirical X), integral_heatP_eq hψ hε μ,
2539      ← Summable.tsum_sub (hsum _ (hnorm1 (empirical X))) (hsum _ (hnorm1 μ))]
2540    refine tsum_congr fun k => ?_
2541    unfold deltaF
2542    rw [← integral_empirical_mode hN X k, ← mul_sub, ← Complex.sub_re, ← sub_mul]
2543  -- Step (ii): Cauchy-Schwarz
2544  set a : ℝ := ((d : ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
2545  set Cl : ℝ := (2 * Real.pi) ^ (-(2 * a)) / c with hCl
2546  have hCl0 : 0 ≤ Cl := by positivity
2547  let f : Lattice d → ℝ := fun k => if k = 0 then 0 else
2548    (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ a * ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k‖
2549  let g : Lattice d → ℝ := fun k => if k = 0 then 0 else
2550    Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (-a) * ‖deltaF X μ k‖
2551  have hf0 : ∀ k, 0 ≤ f k := fun k => by
2552    simp only [f]; split_ifs
2553    · exact le_rfl
2554    · have : 0 ≤ 2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k := mul_nonneg (by positivity) (norm_nonneg _)
2555      exact mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg this _) (norm_nonneg _)
2556  have hg0 : ∀ k, 0 ≤ g k := fun k => by
2557    simp only [g]; split_ifs
2558    · exact le_rfl
2559    · have : 0 ≤ 2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k := mul_nonneg (by positivity) (norm_nonneg _)
2560      exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg (Real.exp_pos _).le (Real.rpow_nonneg this _)) (norm_nonneg _)
2561  have hf2 : ∀ k, f k ^ 2 = homTerm d a ψ k := fun k => by
2562    simp only [f, homTerm]
2563    split_ifs with hk
2564    · simp
2565    · rw [mul_pow, rpow_sq_eq a (z := 2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k)
2566        (mul_nonneg (by positivity) (norm_nonneg _))]
2567  have hfs : Summable fun k => f k ^ 2 := (summable_homTerm hhom).congr (fun k => (hf2 k).symm)
2568  have hcT0 : cT d s ε 0 = 0 := by simp [cT]
2569  have hg2 : ∀ k, g k ^ 2 ≤ Cl * (cT d s ε k * ‖deltaF X μ k‖ ^ 2) := fun k => by
2570    by_cases hk : k = 0
2571    · subst hk
2572      simp [g, hcT0]
2573    · simp only [g, hk, ite_false]
2574      have hm : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm k := norm_nonneg _
2575      have h1 := hcl hε k hk
2576      have h2 : (Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) ^ 2 =
2577          Real.exp (-(2 * ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) := by
2578        rw [← Real.exp_nat_mul]; congr 1; push_cast; ring
2579      have h3 := weight_sq a (x := 2 * Real.pi) (y := Lattice.enorm k) (by positivity) hm
2580      rw [mul_pow, mul_pow, h2, h3]
2581      have h4 : Real.exp (-(2 * ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) * (Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) ^ (-a) ≤
2582          cT d s ε k / c := by
2583        rw [le_div_iff₀ hc]; linarith
2584      have h5 : 0 ≤ ‖deltaF X μ k‖ ^ 2 := sq_nonneg _
2585      calc Real.exp (-(2 * ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) * ((2 * Real.pi) ^ (-(2 * a)) *
2586              (Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) ^ (-a)) * ‖deltaF X μ k‖ ^ 2
2587          = (2 * Real.pi) ^ (-(2 * a)) * (Real.exp (-(2 * ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) *
2588              (Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) ^ (-a)) * ‖deltaF X μ k‖ ^ 2 := by ring
2589        _ ≤ (2 * Real.pi) ^ (-(2 * a)) * (cT d s ε k / c) * ‖deltaF X μ k‖ ^ 2 := by
2590            gcongr
2591        _ = Cl * (cT d s ε k * ‖deltaF X μ k‖ ^ 2) := by rw [hCl]; ring
2592  have hcTs := summable_cT hd hs0 hsd hε
2593  have hcTn := cT_nonneg hd hs0 hsd hε
2594  have hRHS : Summable fun k : Lattice d => Cl * (cT d s ε k * ‖deltaF X μ k‖ ^ 2) := by
2595    refine (Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => mul_nonneg (hcTn k) (sq_nonneg _)) (fun k => ?_)
2596      (hcTs.mul_right 4)).mul_left Cl
2597    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (hcTn k)
2598    have h2 : ‖deltaF X μ k‖ ≤ 2 := delta_norm_le hN X μ k
2599    have h3 := norm_nonneg (deltaF X μ k)
2600    nlinarith
2601  have hgs : Summable fun k => g k ^ 2 :=
2602    Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => sq_nonneg _) hg2 hRHS
2603  obtain ⟨hfgs, hfg⟩ := tsum_mul_le_sqrt hf0 hg0 hfs hgs
2604  have hbound : ∀ k : Lattice d, |Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
2605      (deltaF X μ k * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re| ≤ f k * g k := fun k => by
2606    by_cases hk : k = 0
2607    · subst hk
2608      have : deltaF X μ 0 = 0 := delta_zero hN X μ
2609      simp [this, f]
2610    · simp only [f, g, hk, ite_false]
2611      have hx : 0 < 2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k := by
2612        have := one_le_enorm k hk
2613        have := Real.pi_pos
2614        positivity
2615      have e1 : (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ a * (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (-a) = 1 := by
2616        rw [← Real.rpow_add hx, add_neg_cancel, Real.rpow_zero]
2617      rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
2618      have h1 : |(deltaF X μ k * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re| ≤ ‖deltaF X μ k‖ * ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k‖ :=
2619        (Complex.abs_re_le_norm _).trans (norm_mul _ _).le
2620      calc Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * |(deltaF X μ k * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re|
2621          ≤ Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (‖deltaF X μ k‖ * ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k‖) :=
2622            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (Real.exp_pos _).le
2623        _ = ((2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ a * (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (-a)) *
2624              (Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (‖deltaF X μ k‖ * ‖Torus.fcoef ψ k‖)) := by
2625            rw [e1]; ring
2626        _ = _ := by ring
2627  have hu_sum : Summable fun k : Lattice d => ‖Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
2628      (deltaF X μ k * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re‖ :=
2629    Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun _ => norm_nonneg _) (fun k => by
2630      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact hbound k) hfgs
2631  rw [hstep1]
2632  have hfs2 : ∑' k, f k ^ 2 = homSobolevSq a ψ := by
2633    rw [homSobolevSq_eq_tsum]; exact tsum_congr hf2
2634  have hgs2 : ∑' k, g k ^ 2 ≤ Cl * Esq d s ε X μ := by
2635    calc ∑' k, g k ^ 2 ≤ ∑' k : Lattice d, Cl * (cT d s ε k * ‖deltaF X μ k‖ ^ 2) :=
2636          hgs.tsum_le_tsum hg2 hRHS
2637      _ = Cl * Esq d s ε X μ := by rw [tsum_mul_left]; rfl
2638  calc |∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (deltaF X μ k * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re|
2639      = ‖∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
2640          (deltaF X μ k * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re‖ := (Real.norm_eq_abs _).symm
2641    _ ≤ ∑' k : Lattice d, ‖Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
2642          (deltaF X μ k * Torus.fcoef ψ k).re‖ := norm_tsum_le_tsum_norm hu_sum
2643    _ ≤ ∑' k, f k * g k := hu_sum.tsum_le_tsum (fun k => by rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact hbound k) hfgs
2644    _ ≤ Real.sqrt (∑' k, f k ^ 2) * Real.sqrt (∑' k, g k ^ 2) := hfg
2645    _ ≤ Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq a ψ) * Real.sqrt (Cl * Esq d s ε X μ) := by
2646        rw [hfs2]
2647        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (Real.sqrt_le_sqrt hgs2) (Real.sqrt_nonneg _)
2648

2651theorem abs_integral_sub_le {Pm : Measure (Torus d)} [IsProbabilityMeasure Pm] {f g : Torus d → ℝ}
2652    (hf : Continuous f) (hg : Continuous g) {δ : ℝ} (h : ∀ x, |f x - g x| ≤ δ) :
2653    |∫ x, f x ∂Pm - ∫ x, g x ∂Pm| ≤ δ := by
2654  have hfi : Integrable f Pm := hf.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2655  have hgi : Integrable g Pm := hg.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
2656  rw [← integral_sub hfi hgi, ← Real.norm_eq_abs]
2657  refine (norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const (C := δ) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)).trans ?_
2658  · rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact h x
2659  · simp

2661theorem coercivity_core (hd : 1 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
2662    ∃ C₀ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₀ ∧ 0 < C ∧ ∀ Λ : ℝ, 1 ≤ Λ → ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N →
2663      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
2664      ∀ X : Config d N, X ∉ collisionSet d N →
2665        0 ≤ rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N ∧
2666        ∀ (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (L : ℝ), 0 ≤ L → KernelGrad.IsLipschitz L ψ →
2667          HasFiniteHomSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ →
2668          |∫ x, ψ x ∂(empirical X) - ∫ x, ψ x ∂(withDensityReal ρ)| ≤
2669            C * smearScale d N Λ * L +
2670              C * homSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ *
2671                Real.sqrt (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N) := by
2672  obtain ⟨C₀, hC₀, hE⟩ := coercivity_energy hd hs0 hsd
2673  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := heatP_approx hd
2674  obtain ⟨Cl, hCl0, hCl⟩ := fourier_side_bound hd hs0 hsd
2675  refine ⟨C₀, 2 * M + Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) + 1, hC₀, by positivity, ?_⟩
2676  intro Λ hΛ N hN ρ hρ hρΛ X hX
2677  have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := by omega
2678  have hΛ0 : 0 < Λ := by linarith
2679  have hNΛ : 1 < (N : ℝ) * Λ := by
2680    have : (2 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2681    nlinarith
2682  obtain ⟨hE0, hE1⟩ := hE Λ hΛ N hN ρ hρ hρΛ X hX
2683  set lam := smearScale d N Λ with hlam
2684  have hlam0 : 0 < lam := smearScale_pos (d := d) hN1 hΛ0
2685  have hlam1 : lam < 1 := smearScale_lt_one hd hNΛ
2686  have hε : 0 < lam ^ 2 := by positivity
2687  have hε1 : lam ^ 2 ≤ 1 := by nlinarith
2688  set S := rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N with hS
2689  have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := by linarith
2690  refine ⟨hS0, fun ψ L hL hψ hhom => ?_⟩
2691  haveI := isProb_withDensityReal hρ
2692  haveI := empirical_isProbabilityMeasure (d := d) (by omega : 0 < N) X
2693  set μ := withDensityReal ρ with hμ
2694  have hc := continuous_of_isLipschitz_aux hψ
2695  have hcP := heatP_continuous hc hε
2696  have hδ : ∀ x, |ψ x - heatP d (lam ^ 2) ψ x| ≤ L * (M * lam) := by
2697    intro x
2698    have := hM (lam ^ 2) hε hε1 L ψ hL hψ x
2699    rwa [Real.sqrt_sq hlam0.le] at this
2700  have h1 := abs_integral_sub_le (Pm := empirical X) hc hcP hδ
2701  have h2 := abs_integral_sub_le (Pm := μ) hc hcP hδ
2702  have h3 := hCl hε (by omega : 0 < N) X μ hc hhom
2703  have h4 : Real.sqrt (Cl * Esq d s (lam ^ 2) X μ) ≤ Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) * Real.sqrt S := by
2704    rw [← Real.sqrt_mul (by positivity)]
2705    apply Real.sqrt_le_sqrt
2706    nlinarith
2707  have hhom0 : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) := Real.sqrt_nonneg _
2708  have h5 : Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * Real.sqrt (Cl * Esq d s (lam ^ 2) X μ) ≤
2709      Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * (Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) * Real.sqrt S) :=
2710    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h4 hhom0
2711  -- decomposition
2712  have hdec : ∫ x, ψ x ∂(empirical X) - ∫ x, ψ x ∂μ =
2713      (∫ x, ψ x ∂(empirical X) - ∫ x, heatP d (lam ^ 2) ψ x ∂(empirical X)) -
2714        (∫ x, ψ x ∂μ - ∫ x, heatP d (lam ^ 2) ψ x ∂μ) +
2715        (∫ x, heatP d (lam ^ 2) ψ x ∂(empirical X) - ∫ x, heatP d (lam ^ 2) ψ x ∂μ) := by ring
2716  rw [hdec]
2717  have hhomS : homSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ = Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) := rfl
2718  rw [hhomS]
2719  have hLM : 0 ≤ L * (M * lam) := by positivity
2720  have hsq0 : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) := Real.sqrt_nonneg _
2721  have hS' : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt S := Real.sqrt_nonneg _
2722  have hAbs : |(∫ x, ψ x ∂(empirical X) - ∫ x, heatP d (lam ^ 2) ψ x ∂(empirical X)) -
2723        (∫ x, ψ x ∂μ - ∫ x, heatP d (lam ^ 2) ψ x ∂μ) +
2724        (∫ x, heatP d (lam ^ 2) ψ x ∂(empirical X) - ∫ x, heatP d (lam ^ 2) ψ x ∂μ)| ≤
2725      L * (M * lam) + L * (M * lam) +
2726        Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * (Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) * Real.sqrt S) := by
2727    refine (abs_add_le _ _).trans ?_
2728    refine add_le_add ((abs_sub _ _).trans (add_le_add h1 h2)) (h3.trans h5)
2729  refine hAbs.trans ?_
2730  have hmid : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * Real.sqrt S := mul_nonneg hhom0 hS'
2731  have hmid2 : Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * (Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) * Real.sqrt S) ≤
2732      (2 * M + Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) + 1) * Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * Real.sqrt S := by
2733    have : Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * (Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) * Real.sqrt S) =
2734        Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) * (Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * Real.sqrt S) := by ring
2735    rw [this]
2736    have h6 : (2 * M + Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) + 1) * Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * Real.sqrt S =
2737        (2 * M + Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) + 1) * (Real.sqrt (homSobolevSq (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ) * Real.sqrt S) := by ring
2738    rw [h6]
2739    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) hmid
2740  have hlin : L * (M * lam) + L * (M * lam) ≤ (2 * M + Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) + 1) * lam * L := by
2741    have : 0 ≤ (Real.sqrt (2 * Cl) + 1) * lam * L := by positivity
2742    nlinarith
2743  linarith
2744
2745end CoercHeat
2746
2747
2748end CoercFourier

2750
2751end CoercAux

2753/-- Proposition 3.34 `M:B:prop:hrs-periodic-coercivity` (source lines 2865-2898).  Let `d ≥ 1`,
2754`0 ≤ s < d`, and the zero-mean periodic kernel `ĝ(k) = c_{d,s}(2π|k|)^{s-d}` (`k ≠ 0`),
2755`c_{d,s} > 0` (this is `periodicRiesz d s`, with the normalisation `g = -log|x| + O(1)` when
2756`s = 0`, ISSUES §B K1).  Let `μ = ρ dx` be a probability density with `‖μ‖_∞ ≤ Λ`, `Λ ≥ 1`,
2757`X_N` pairwise distinct, `N ≥ 2`, `λ = (NΛ)^{-1/d}`, `a_N^Λ = Λ^{s/d} N^{s/d-1}`.  There are
2758constants `C₀, C` depending only on `d, s` (and the kernel normalisation) such that, with
2759`o_N(Λ) = 1_{s=0} log(NΛ)/(2dN) + C₀ a_N^Λ`:
2760`F_N(X_N,μ) + o_N(Λ) ≥ 0` (`B:eq:hrs-periodic-coercivity-lower`), and for every Lipschitz real
2761`ψ` (constant `L ≥ 0`) with finite `Ḣ^{(d-s)/2}` seminorm,
2762`|∫ ψ dρ_N| ≤ C λ ‖∇ψ‖_∞ + C ‖ψ‖_{Ḣ^{(d-s)/2}} (F_N + o_N(Λ))^{1/2}`
2763(`B:eq:hrs-periodic-coercivity-test`), `ρ_N = N⁻¹∑δ_{x_i} - μ`.  The constants are independent
2764of the configuration (including its smallest pair distance); "increasing `C₀` preserves both
2765statements" is `periodic_coercivity_mono`.
2766
2767PLAN (source proof 2900-3054): truncate the heat-kernel representation of `g`
2768(`heatRiesz d s ε`, `HRS/Kernel/Riesz.lean`; `heatRiesz_ge`, `periodicRiesz_ge`,
2769`theta_nonneg`, `psi_ge` in `HRS/Energy/Modulated.lean` are the positivity inputs) at scale
2770`λ`, keep the smooth positive quadratic form `∬ g_λ d(ρ_N)^{⊗2} ≥ 0`, and estimate the difference
2771with `F_N` explicitly including the deleted empirical diagonal (this produces `o_N(Λ)`:
2772the diagonal term `N⁻¹ g_λ(0) ≍ N⁻¹λ^{-s}` for `s>0`, `N⁻¹ log(1/λ)` for `s = 0`, and the
2773`λ^{2}` Lipschitz/`λ^{d-s}` smearing errors bounded by `Λ`-weighted integrals); for the test bound,
2774split `ψ = ψ * φ_λ + (ψ - ψ*φ_λ)` and use Cauchy–Schwarz in Fourier against the positive form
2775(`B:eq:hrs-heat-sharp-control` with `η = λ`) and the Lipschitz cutoff bound
2776`B:eq:hrs-cutoff-lipschitz`. -/
2777theorem periodic_coercivity (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
2778    ∃ C₀ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₀ ∧ 0 < C ∧
2779      ∀ (Λ : ℝ), 1 ≤ Λ → ∀ (N : ℕ), 2 ≤ N →
2780      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
2781      ∀ X : Config d N, X ∉ collisionSet d N →
2782        0 ≤ rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N ∧
2783        ∀ (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (L : ℝ), 0 ≤ L → KernelGrad.IsLipschitz L ψ →
2784          HasFiniteHomSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ →
2785          |∫ x, ψ x ∂(empirical X) - ∫ x, ψ x ∂(withDensityReal ρ)| ≤
2786            C * smearScale d N Λ * L +
2787              C * homSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ *
2788                Real.sqrt (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N) := by
2789  obtain ⟨C₀, C, hC₀, hC, h⟩ := CoercAux.coercivity_core hd hs0 hsd
2790  exact ⟨C₀, C, hC₀, hC, h⟩

2792/-- Proposition 3.34, last sentence (line 2897): "increasing `C₀` preserves both statements" —
2793the correction `o_N(Λ)` is monotone in `C₀` (`Λ ≥ 1`), and both the lower bound and the test bound
2794are monotone in the correction.  PLAN: `Real.rpow_nonneg`, `Real.sqrt_le_sqrt`, `mul_le_mul`. -/
2795theorem periodic_coercivity_mono {s C₀ C₀' Λ : ℝ} (hC : C₀ ≤ C₀') (hΛ : 0 ≤ Λ) (N : ℕ)
2796    (F a b c : ℝ) (hc : 0 ≤ c) :
2797    oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N ≤ oNΛ d s C₀' Λ N ∧
2798    (0 ≤ F + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N → 0 ≤ F + oNΛ d s C₀' Λ N) ∧
2799    (0 ≤ F + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N → a ≤ b + c * Real.sqrt (F + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N) →
2800      a ≤ b + c * Real.sqrt (F + oNΛ d s C₀' Λ N)) := by
2801  have hmono : oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N ≤ oNΛ d s C₀' Λ N := by
2802    unfold oNΛ
2803    have ha : 0 ≤ Λ ^ (s / d) := Real.rpow_nonneg hΛ _
2804    have hb : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := Real.rpow_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg N) _
2805    have h1 : C₀ * Λ ^ (s / d) ≤ C₀' * Λ ^ (s / d) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hC ha
2806    have h2 : C₀ * Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) ≤
2807        C₀' * Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h1 hb
2808    linarith
2809  refine ⟨hmono, fun h => by linarith, fun h1 h2 => ?_⟩
2810  have hs : Real.sqrt (F + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N) ≤ Real.sqrt (F + oNΛ d s C₀' Λ N) :=
2811    Real.sqrt_le_sqrt (by linarith)
2812  have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hs hc
2813  linarith

2831/-- Proposition 3.33, item (ii) (control of linear statistics, `B:eq:coercivity-test`, lines

2838      ∀ (Λ : ℝ), 1 ≤ Λ → ∀ (N : ℕ), 2 ≤ N →

2853/-- `o_N(Λ)` is monotone in `C₀` (for `Λ ≥ 0`). -/
2854theorem oNΛ_mono_C₀ {s C₀ C₀' Λ : ℝ} (hC : C₀ ≤ C₀') (hΛ : 0 ≤ Λ) (N : ℕ) :
2855    oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N ≤ oNΛ d s C₀' Λ N := by
2856  unfold oNΛ
2857  have h1 : 0 ≤ Λ ^ (s / d) := Real.rpow_nonneg hΛ _
2858  have h2 : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := Real.rpow_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg N) _
2859  have h3 : 0 ≤ Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := mul_nonneg h1 h2
2860  have : C₀ * Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) ≤ C₀' * Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := by
2861    rw [mul_assoc, mul_assoc]
2862    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hC h3
2863  linarith
