2870/-- Propositions 3.32 and 3.33 with a common `C₀` ("with `C₀` as fixed above", line 2843): the
2871commutator bound, the lower bound and the test bound hold simultaneously for one `C₀ = C₀(d,s)`.
2872PLAN: take the maximum of the two `C₀`'s from `commutator_estimate` and `periodic_coercivity`
2873and use `periodic_coercivity_mono` (the commutator bound is monotone in `C₀` as well since
2874`C K ≥ 0`). -/
2875theorem commutator_coercivity_common (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
2876    ∃ C₀ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₀ ∧ 0 < C ∧
2877      ∀ (Λ : ℝ), 1 ≤ Λ → ∀ (N : ℕ), 2 ≤ N →
2878      ∀ ρ : Torus d → ℝ, IsProbDensity ρ → (∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) →
2879      ∀ X : Config d N, X ∉ collisionSet d N →
2880        0 ≤ rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N ∧
2881        (∀ (v : Torus d → Euc d) (K : ℝ), HasTransportBound d s v K →
2882          |commQuantity d s v X (withDensityReal ρ)| ≤
2883            C * K * (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N)) ∧
2884        (∀ (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (L : ℝ), 0 ≤ L → KernelGrad.IsLipschitz L ψ →
2885          HasFiniteHomSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ →
2886          |∫ x, ψ x ∂(empirical X) - ∫ x, ψ x ∂(withDensityReal ρ)| ≤
2887            C * L * smearScale d N Λ +
2888              C * homSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ *
2889                Real.sqrt (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N)) := by
2890  obtain ⟨A₀, A, hA₀, hA, hcomm⟩ := commutator_estimate hd hs0 hsd
2891  obtain ⟨B₀, B, hB₀, hB, hcoer⟩ := periodic_coercivity hd hs0 hsd
2892  refine ⟨max A₀ B₀, max A B, le_max_of_le_left hA₀, lt_max_of_lt_left hA, ?_⟩
2893  intro Λ hΛ N hN ρ hρ hρΛ X hX
2894  have hΛ0 : 0 ≤ Λ := by linarith
2895  obtain ⟨hlow, htest⟩ := hcoer Λ hΛ N hN ρ hρ hρΛ X hX
2896  have hoB : oNΛ d s B₀ Λ N ≤ oNΛ d s (max A₀ B₀) Λ N :=
2897    oNΛ_mono_C₀ (le_max_right _ _) hΛ0 N
2898  have hoA : oNΛ d s A₀ Λ N ≤ oNΛ d s (max A₀ B₀) Λ N :=
2899    oNΛ_mono_C₀ (le_max_left _ _) hΛ0 N
2900  have hlow' : 0 ≤ rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s (max A₀ B₀) Λ N := by
2901    linarith
2902  refine ⟨hlow', ?_, ?_⟩
2903  · intro v K hK
2904    have hK0 := hK.nonneg
2905    have h1 := (hcomm Λ hΛ N hN ρ hρ hρΛ X hX v K hK).2
2906    refine h1.trans ?_
2907    have hAK : 0 ≤ A * K := mul_nonneg hA.le hK0
2908    calc A * K * (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s A₀ Λ N)
2909        ≤ A * K * (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s (max A₀ B₀) Λ N) :=
2910          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hAK
2911      _ ≤ max A B * K * (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s (max A₀ B₀) Λ N) := by
2912          apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hlow'
2913          exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (le_max_left _ _) hK0
2914  · intro ψ L hL hψ hfin
2915    refine (htest ψ L hL hψ hfin).trans ?_
2916    have hsm : 0 ≤ smearScale d N Λ :=
2917      Real.rpow_nonneg (mul_nonneg (Nat.cast_nonneg N) hΛ0) _
2918    have hhs : 0 ≤ homSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ := Real.sqrt_nonneg _
2919    have hsq : Real.sqrt (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s B₀ Λ N) ≤
2920        Real.sqrt (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s (max A₀ B₀) Λ N) :=
2921      Real.sqrt_le_sqrt (by linarith)
2922    have hAB : B ≤ max A B := le_max_right _ _
2923    have hsq0 := Real.sqrt_nonneg (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s B₀ Λ N)
2924    have e1 : B * smearScale d N Λ * L ≤ max A B * L * smearScale d N Λ := by
2925      nlinarith [mul_nonneg hsm hL]
2926    have e2 : B * homSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ *
2927        Real.sqrt (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s B₀ Λ N) ≤
2928        max A B * homSobolev (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) ψ *
2929        Real.sqrt (rieszEnergy d s X (withDensityReal ρ) + oNΛ d s (max A₀ B₀) Λ N) := by
2930      apply mul_le_mul _ hsq hsq0 (mul_nonneg (hA.le.trans (le_max_left _ _)) hhs)
2931      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hAB hhs
2932    linarith
2933
2934/-! ### Remark 3.35: use with a fixed density bound -/
2935
2936/-- Remark 3.35 `M:B:rem:hrs-coercivity-fixed-density` (source lines 3056-3066), first claim:
2937for a fixed `Λ`, `o_N(Λ) ≤ C₀' a_N` with `a_N = (1 + 1_{s=0} log N) N^{s/d-1}` and a constant `C₀'`
2938depending on `Λ` (and `C₀, d, s`); thus a single corrected energy `F_N + C₀' a_N` can be used
2939throughout any trajectory with this density bound (by `periodic_coercivity_mono`).
2940PLAN: `log(NΛ) = log N + log Λ`, `log Λ/(2dN) ≤ log Λ · N^{s/d-1}` since `s/d - 1 ≥ -1`,
2941`Λ^{s/d} ≤ Λ`. -/
2942theorem oNΛ_le_aN (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (C₀ : ℝ) (hC₀ : 0 ≤ C₀)
2943    (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : 1 ≤ Λ) :
2944    ∃ C₀' : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₀' ∧ ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N → oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N ≤ C₀' * aN d s N := by
2945  have hd' : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
2946  have hΛ0 : (0 : ℝ) < Λ := by linarith
2947  have hlΛ : 0 ≤ Real.log Λ := Real.log_nonneg hΛ
2948  have hpos : 0 ≤ (1 + Real.log Λ) / (2 * d) := div_nonneg (by linarith) (by positivity)
2949  have hCΛ : 0 ≤ C₀ * Λ := mul_nonneg hC₀ hΛ0.le
2950  refine ⟨C₀ * Λ + (1 + Real.log Λ) / (2 * d), by linarith, ?_⟩
2951  intro N hN
2952  have hN' : (2 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
2953  have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
2954  have hx : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := Real.rpow_nonneg hN0.le _
2955  have hlN : 0 ≤ Real.log N := Real.log_nonneg (by linarith)
2956  by_cases hs : s = 0
2957  · subst hs
2958    have hxN : (N : ℝ) ^ ((0 : ℝ) / d - 1) = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by
2959      rw [zero_div, zero_sub, Real.rpow_neg_one]
2960    have hinv : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := inv_nonneg.mpr hN0.le
2961    unfold oNΛ aN
2962    rw [hxN, Real.log_mul hN0.ne' hΛ0.ne']
2963    simp only [if_true, zero_div, Real.rpow_zero, one_mul, mul_one, add_zero]
2964    have hA : 0 ≤ C₀ * (Λ * (1 + Real.log N) - 1) := by
2965      apply mul_nonneg hC₀
2966      nlinarith
2967    have hB : 0 ≤ (1 / (2 * d)) * (1 + Real.log Λ * Real.log N) := by
2968      apply mul_nonneg (by positivity)
2969      have := mul_nonneg hlΛ hlN
2970      linarith
2971    have key : (C₀ * Λ + (1 + Real.log Λ) / (2 * d)) * ((1 + Real.log N) * (N : ℝ)⁻¹)
2972        - ((Real.log N + Real.log Λ) / (2 * d * N) + C₀ * (N : ℝ)⁻¹)
2973        = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * (C₀ * (Λ * (1 + Real.log N) - 1) + (1 / (2 * d)) * (1 + Real.log Λ * Real.log N)) := by
2974      field_simp
2975      ring
2976    nlinarith [mul_nonneg hinv (add_nonneg hA hB)]
2977  · have hsd1 : s / d ≤ 1 := (div_le_one hd').mpr hsd.le
2978    have ha : Λ ^ (s / d) ≤ Λ := by
2979      calc Λ ^ (s / d) ≤ Λ ^ (1 : ℝ) := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le (by linarith) hsd1
2980        _ = Λ := Real.rpow_one Λ
2981    unfold oNΛ aN
2982    simp only [if_neg hs, zero_add, add_zero, one_mul]
2983    have h1 : C₀ * Λ ^ (s / d) ≤ C₀ * Λ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ha hC₀
2984    have h2 : C₀ * Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) ≤ C₀ * Λ * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) :=
2985      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h1 hx
2986    have h3 : 0 ≤ (1 + Real.log Λ) / (2 * d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := mul_nonneg hpos hx
2987    nlinarith
2988
2989/-- Remark 3.35, second claim (lines 3062-3066): the correction `o_N(Λ)` has the same logarithmic
2990coefficient `1_{s=0} log N/(2dN)` as `o_N` of `H:eq:energy-completion` (`HRS.oN`), and only the
2991fixed constant `C₀` of the nonlogarithmic part may need to be enlarged: for fixed `Λ ≥ 1` there is
2992`C₀'` with `o_N(Λ) ≤ o_N(C₀')` for all `N ≥ 2`; at `Λ = 1` the two agree.
2993PLAN: as `oNΛ_le_aN`; `oNΛ_one` is `Real.rpow` of `1` and `mul_one`. -/
2994theorem oNΛ_le_oN (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (C₀ : ℝ) (hC₀ : 0 ≤ C₀)
2995    (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : 1 ≤ Λ) :
2996    (∀ N : ℕ, oNΛ d s C₀ 1 N = oN d s C₀ N) ∧
2997    ∃ C₀' : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₀' ∧ ∀ N : ℕ, 2 ≤ N → oNΛ d s C₀ Λ N ≤ oN d s C₀' N := by
2998  have hd' : (0 : ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
2999  have hΛ0 : (0 : ℝ) < Λ := by linarith
3000  have hlΛ : 0 ≤ Real.log Λ := Real.log_nonneg hΛ
3001  have hpos : 0 ≤ Real.log Λ / (2 * d) := div_nonneg hlΛ (by positivity)
3002  have hCΛ : 0 ≤ C₀ * Λ := mul_nonneg hC₀ hΛ0.le
3003  refine ⟨fun N => ?_, C₀ * Λ + Real.log Λ / (2 * d), by linarith, ?_⟩
3004  · unfold oNΛ oN
3005    simp only [Real.one_rpow, mul_one, one_mul]
3006    ring
3007  intro N hN
3008  have hN' : (2 : ℝ) ≤ N := by exact_mod_cast hN
3009  have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < N := by linarith
3010  have hx : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := Real.rpow_nonneg hN0.le _
3011  by_cases hs : s = 0
3012  · subst hs
3013    have hxN : (N : ℝ) ^ ((0 : ℝ) / d - 1) = (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by
3014      rw [zero_div, zero_sub, Real.rpow_neg_one]
3015    have hinv : 0 ≤ (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := inv_nonneg.mpr hN0.le
3016    unfold oNΛ oN
3017    rw [hxN, Real.log_mul hN0.ne' hΛ0.ne']
3018    simp only [if_true, zero_div, Real.rpow_zero, one_mul, mul_one]
3019    have hC : 0 ≤ C₀ * (Λ - 1) := mul_nonneg hC₀ (by linarith)
3020    have e : (Real.log N + Real.log Λ) / (2 * d * N) =
3021        Real.log N / (2 * d * N) + Real.log Λ / (2 * d) * (N : ℝ)⁻¹ := by ring
3022    rw [e]
3023    nlinarith [mul_nonneg hinv hC]
3024  · have hsd1 : s / d ≤ 1 := (div_le_one hd').mpr hsd.le
3025    have ha : Λ ^ (s / d) ≤ Λ := by
3026      calc Λ ^ (s / d) ≤ Λ ^ (1 : ℝ) := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le (by linarith) hsd1
3027        _ = Λ := Real.rpow_one Λ
3028    unfold oNΛ oN
3029    simp only [if_neg hs, zero_add, add_zero]
3030    have h1 : C₀ * Λ ^ (s / d) ≤ C₀ * Λ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ha hC₀
3031    have h2 : C₀ * Λ ^ (s / d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) ≤ C₀ * Λ * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) :=
3032      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h1 hx
3033    have h3 : 0 ≤ Real.log Λ / (2 * d) * (N : ℝ) ^ (s / d - 1) := mul_nonneg hpos hx
3034    nlinarith

3036/-! ### Energies along the particle dynamics -/
3037
3038/-- `F_N(X_N^t, μ^t) + o_N(Λ)` along a particle path and a mean-field density. -/
3039def correctedEnergy (D : DynData d) (C₀ Λ : ℝ) (ρ : ℝ → Torus d → ℝ) (X : Config d N) (t : ℝ) :
3040    ℝ :=
3041  rieszEnergy d D.s X (withDensityReal (ρ t)) + oNΛ d D.s C₀ Λ N
3042
3043/-- The self-force `(∇k * ρ_N)°(x_i) = N⁻¹ ∑_{j≠i} ∇k(x_i - x_j) - ∇k*μ(x_i)` (Proposition 3.38,
3044line 3158; `a_i` in the proof of Proposition 3.37, line 3129) for a force field `G = ∇k`, with
3045`∇k*μ` by the convention convolution `convGradConv` (ISSUES §B K3; for bounded `G` it is the plain
3046convolution). -/
3047def selfForce (G : Torus d → Euc d) (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (X : Config d N) (i : Fin N) : Euc d :=
3048  (1 / (N : ℝ)) • ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i, G (X i - X j) - convGradConv G ρ (X i)
3049
3050/-- The off-diagonal pairing `∬_{𝔻²∖△} k(x-y) d(ρ_N)^{⊗2}` of a difference kernel. -/
3051def offDiagDiffPairing (k : Torus d → ℝ) (X : Config d N) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) : ℝ :=
3052  offDiagPairing (fun x y => if x = y then 0 else k (x - y)) X μ
3053
3054/-- Normalised relative entropy `𝖧_N(f | Q) = N⁻¹ H(f | Q)` (used at line 3102; see ISSUES §C). -/
3055def normRelEntropy (N : ℕ) (f Q : Measure (Config d N)) : ℝ≥0∞ := relEntropy f Q / N
3056
3057/-- The modulated free energy `𝖤_N(f, μ) = β⁻¹ 𝖧_N(f | μ^{⊗N}) + ∫ F_N(X_N, μ) f(dX_N)` (used
3058in Propositions 3.36(C2) and 3.41 without a definition in the main paper; see ISSUES §C), with
3059`β⁻¹ = invβ`; a real number meaningful under `MFEFinite`. -/
3060def modulatedFreeEnergy (d : ℕ) (s invβ : ℝ) {N : ℕ} (f : Measure (Config d N))
3061    (μ : Measure (Torus d)) : ℝ :=
3062  invβ * (normRelEntropy N f (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ)).toReal +
3063    ∫ X, rieszEnergy d s X μ ∂f
3064
3065/-- `𝖤_N(f, μ) < ∞`: finite relative entropy and integrable interaction energy. -/
3066def MFEFinite (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) {N : ℕ} (f : Measure (Config d N)) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) :
3067    Prop :=
3068  relEntropy f (Measure.pi fun _ : Fin N => μ) ≠ ∞ ∧ Integrable (fun X => rieszEnergy d s X μ) f
3069
3070/-- The transport field `u_V^t = β⁻¹ ∇ log μ^t + ∇g*μ^t + ∇V` of `B:eq:uV-periodic` (line 3208). -/
3071def mfeTransport (D : DynData d) (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (x : Torus d) : Euc d :=
3072  D.invβ • Torus.grad (fun y => Real.log (ρ y)) x +
3073    convGradConv (periodicRieszGrad d D.s) ρ x + Torus.grad D.V x
3074
3075/-- The standing hypotheses of Proposition 3.41 `M:B:prop:mfe-uniform` (source lines 3204-3210):
3076`𝕋^d`, `0 ≤ s < d` (in `DynData`), `M = -I`, `β_N < ∞` (`invβ > 0`), `V ∈ C^{m_*+2}`, the selected
3077initial law has finite Gibbs entropy `B:eq:selected-initial-gibbs-entropy` (relative entropy with
3078respect to the normalised Gibbs measure `G_{N,β_N,V}`, `B:eq:gibbs-entropy-measure-convention`,
3079line 12014) and `𝖤_N(f_N^0, μ°) < ∞`; `μ_{β_N}` is an `(m_*+2, Λ)`-regular mean-field solution on
3080`[0,T]` with datum `μ° = ρ 0` and `inf μ_{β_N} ≥ lam₀ > 0`; `X` is the selected particle process
3081(`B:ass:sde`(a): the collision-free strong solution, with `X_0` independent of the noise).  The field `filtered` (statement
3082repair, ISSUES.md, entry "Filtered strong solutions: follow-up repairs") is the strong-solution
3083adaptedness of `B:ass:sde` (a Brownian filtration carrying `W` to which `X` is adapted) that Proposition B.15
3084`sde_entropy_law` (the first input of `mfe_uniform`) takes and `IsStrongSolution` (measurability only, ISSUES D1) does
3085not record. -/
3086structure MFEHyp {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) (D : DynData d) (N : ℕ) (T Λ lam₀ : ℝ)
3087    (ρ : ℝ → Torus d → ℝ) (X₀ : Ω → Config d N) (W : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d))
3088    (X : ℝ≥0 → Ω → Config d N) : Prop where
3089  gradient : D.M = -1
3090  pos_temp : 0 < D.invβ
3091  V_reg : Torus.IsCk (PDE.mStar d + 2) D.V
3092  mf : IsMeanFieldSolution D T ρ
3093  mf_reg : IsRegularMF (PDE.mStar d + 2) Λ T ρ
3094  Λ_ge : 1 ≤ Λ
3095  pos_density : 0 < lam₀ ∧ ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, ∀ x, lam₀ ≤ ρ t x
3096  gibbs_entropy : relEntropy (P.map X₀) (canonicalGibbs (periodicRiesz d D.s) D.V N (1 / D.invβ)) ≠ ∞
3097  mfe_init : MFEFinite d D.s (P.map X₀) (withDensityReal (ρ 0))
3098  brownian : IsVectorBrownian P W
3099  indep : ProbabilityTheory.IndepFun X₀ (fun ω => fun t : ℝ≥0 => W t ω) P
3100  sol : IsStrongSolution P D T X₀ W X
3101  filtered : ∃ (ℱ : MeasureTheory.Filtration ℝ ‹MeasurableSpace Ω›)
3102    (W' : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N × Fin d) → ℝ), IsFilteredSolution P ℱ W' W X
3103
3104/-! ### Proposition 3.36: dynamical modulated-energy bounds -/
3105
3106/-- Proposition 3.36 `M:B:prop:gronwall-catalog` (source lines 3078-3111), item (C1) *zero
3107temperature* (lines 3082-3094): `β_N = ∞` (`invβ = 0`), `𝖲 = ½(M + Mᵀ) ≤ 0`, `μ_{β_N}` an
3108`(r,Λ)`-regular mean-field solution, `r ≥ 3`, and the effective transport
3109`u^t = M ∇g*μ^t - ∇V` with transport seminorm `‖u^t‖_* ≤ Λ'` uniformly on `[0,T]` (the
3110hypothesis of the applicable commutator estimate; for Coulomb and super-Coulomb the seminorm is
3111`‖∇u^t‖_∞`, in the strict sub-Coulomb case it includes the fractional term, which is what
3112`HasTransportBound` encodes).  Then, pathwise,
3113`F_N(X_N^t, μ^t) + o_N ≤ e^{CTΛ'} (F_N(X_N^0, μ^0) + o_N)` for `0 ≤ t ≤ T`
3114(`B:eq:zero-temperature-energy-bound`), with `C₀, C` depending on `d, s` only.
3115
3116PLAN: at zero temperature the particle system is a pathwise ODE (`IsStrongSolution.lift` with
3117`invβ = 0`); `t ↦ F_N(X^t, μ^t)` is absolutely continuous with derivative
3118`½ Comm_N(u^t; X^t, μ^t) + N⁻¹ ∑_i a_iᵀ 𝖲 a_i` (the deterministic case of
3119`B:eq:coulomb-energy-identity`/`comparison_kernel_identity`: differentiate the three terms of
3120`F_N` along the flow using the mean-field weak formulation `IsMeanFieldSolution.weak` for the
3121`μ`-terms, `convGradConv` for the force and the symmetrisation of `interactionPairing`), the second
3122term is `≤ 0` by `SymNonpos`, the first is `≤ ½ C Λ' (F_N + o_N)` by `commutator_estimate`
3123(`IsRegularMF` gives `‖μ^t‖_∞ ≤ Λ`), and Grönwall (`gronwall_bound`/`norm_le_gronwallBound_of_norm_deriv_right_le`
3124in Mathlib) closes. 
3125
3126Statement repair 2026-10-17 (ISSUES "G336"): added `Torus.IsCk 2 D.V` (V otherwise enters only through the junk
3127`Torus.grad D.V`; the proof uses Prop 3.38, which needs it; TeX standing assumption V ∈ C^{m_V}; same as SPEC-C0 Defect 2). -/
3128theorem gronwall_zero_temperature (hd : 1 ≤ d) {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
3129    ∃ C₀ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C₀ ∧ 0 < C ∧
3130      ∀ (D : DynData d), D.s = s → D.invβ = 0 → Torus.SymNonpos D.M → Torus.IsCk 2 D.V →
3131      ∀ (N : ℕ), 2 ≤ N → ∀ (T : ℝ), 0 ≤ T →
3132      ∀ (ρ : ℝ → Torus d → ℝ) (r : ℕ) (Λ : ℝ), 3 ≤ r → 1 ≤ Λ →
3133        IsMeanFieldSolution D T ρ → IsRegularMF r Λ T ρ →
3134      ∀ Λ' : ℝ, (∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, HasTransportBound d s (meanFieldDrift D (ρ t)) Λ') →
3135      ∀ {Ω : Type} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
3136        (X₀ : Ω → Config d N) (W : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)) (X : ℝ≥0 → Ω → Config d N),

3292even kernel `h`, `F_{N,h}` the modulated energy with `g` replaced by `h`, and along dynamics

3296+ β_N⁻¹ E[∬_{𝔻²∖△} Δh d(ρ_N^t)^{⊗2}]`, stated in integrated form on `[0,t]`, `t ≤ T`, under the
3297integrability of the microscopic term that the source presupposes (the other two terms are
3298bounded for smooth `h`).  "In particular, for `h = g_λ` the middle term is a force–kernel mismatch

3341/-- The quadratic form of `M` only sees `Sym M = (M + Mᵀ)/2`. -/
3342theorem inner_matVec_symPart (M : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℝ) (a : Euc d) :
3343    inner ℝ a (Torus.matVec M a) = inner ℝ a (Torus.matVec (Torus.symPart M) a) := by
3344  have h1 : Torus.matVec M.transpose a = (Matrix.toEuclideanLin M).adjoint a := by
3345    rw [← Matrix.toEuclideanLin_conjTranspose_eq_adjoint, Matrix.conjTranspose_eq_transpose_of_trivial]
3346    rfl
3347  have h2 : inner ℝ a ((Matrix.toEuclideanLin M).adjoint a) = inner ℝ (Matrix.toEuclideanLin M a) a :=
3348    LinearMap.adjoint_inner_right _ _ _
3349  have h3 : Torus.matVec (Torus.symPart M) a =
3350      (1 / 2 : ℝ) • (Torus.matVec M a + Torus.matVec M.transpose a) := by
3351    unfold Torus.matVec Torus.symPart
3352    simp [map_add, map_smul]
3353  rw [h3, inner_smul_right, inner_add_right, h1, h2]
3354  have e : inner ℝ (Matrix.toEuclideanLin M a) a = inner ℝ a (Torus.matVec M a) :=
3355    real_inner_comm _ _
3356  rw [e]
3357  ring

3361quadratic form `N⁻¹ ∑_i a_iᵀ 𝖲 a_i`, `𝖲 = ½(M + Mᵀ)`, `a_i = (∇g*ρ_N)°(x_i)` (also used at line
33623175 and in `B:eq:coulomb-energy-identity`).  The rest of the remark (the singularity of
3363`Δg ≍ |x|^{-s-2}` for `s > d-2`, the unresolved near-field mixed product for `h = g_λ`, and the
3364role of the entropy for gradient flows) is discussion without a formal claim.

3367`G = ∇g`) since only the algebra of `M` enters. -/
3368theorem microTerm_self_kernel (D : DynData d) (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (X : Config d N)
3369    (G : Torus d → Euc d) :
3370    (1 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i, inner ℝ (selfForce G ρ X i) (Torus.matVec D.M (selfForce G ρ X i)) =
3371      (1 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i, inner ℝ (selfForce G ρ X i)
3372        (Torus.matVec (Torus.symPart D.M) (selfForce G ρ X i)) := by
3373  congr 1

3375  exact inner_matVec_symPart D.M _
